# UX-аудит фронтенда Choros

> Системный прогон через MCP `ux-patterns` (review_code + WCAG check_accessibility AA) + экспертная сверка с дизайн-системой.
> Дата: 2026-06-29 · Ветка: `dev` · Файлов проверено: **48** · Находок: **383**

| Severity | Кол-во |
|---|---|
| 🔴 critical | 3 |
| 🟠 high | 88 |
| 🟡 medium | 161 |
| ⚪ low | 131 |

---

## Резюме

The Choros frontend is functional in its happy paths but carries pervasive, systemic UX debt concentrated in three classes: design-system bypass, accessibility, and honesty of feedback. Across 48 files and 383 findings (3 critical, 88 high), the same defects recur in nearly every area because shared primitives are either missing or routinely ignored — controls are hand-rolled as raw <input>/<select>/<button> instead of the kit <Field>/<Select>/<Button> in all six areas, loading/empty/error states are bespoke <div>s rather than the kit state primitives, and there is no app-wired toast system so action outcomes are silently swallowed, shown via blocking window.alert(), or left as stale inline banners. The most urgent issue is that the brandbook showcase — the single visual consistency reference for the whole app — crashes on load (missing ReactDOM import), so the design system has no enforceable source of truth. Accessibility is broken on the primary authoring surfaces: the BPMN canvas, the form-designer drag-and-drop, command palette, tabs, trees and accordions are all keyboard-inaccessible or expose fake ARIA roles, and custom clickable <div>/<tr> rows lock out keyboard and screen-reader users (WCAG 2.1.1/4.1.2 failures everywhere). Most dangerously, destructive and irreversible operations — revoking all of an employee's rights, unbinding encrypted API keys, deleting outcomes/rules, publishing/deploying to the live engine — fire on a single click with no ConfirmDialog and no undo, despite the kit shipping ConfirmDialog for exactly this. Dev-jargon (UUIDs, JSON dumps, raw HTTP codes, English enums, ISO timestamps, slugs) leaks into this Russian no-code B2B UI in every area, undermining the product's core promise to non-technical operators. Fix first: repair the showcase, ship a global toast/feedback provider and gate destructive actions behind ConfirmDialog, then drive the systemic primitive-adoption and accessibility sweeps.

---

## Топ-15 приоритетных тем (сквозные)

### 1. 🔴 Destructive and irreversible actions fire on one click with no confirmation or undo

- **Severity:** critical · **Усилие:** L
- **Охват:** rights (ra-intents/criticality/role-editor/sod), core-work (schema/inbox/process-editor/records), ai (llm-connections/dmn/prompt/agents), observability (reports publish), canvas (delete outcome) — ~15 files across 5 areas
- **Проблема:** High-blast-radius operations commit instantly with no ConfirmDialog and no undo: «Уволить» revokes ALL of an employee's grants, «Срочно отозвать» kills a running agent, unbinding an encrypted API key breaks dependent agents, «Опубликовать»/«Удалить исход»/«Удалить правило» and live-engine deploys all destroy state on a stray click. Some flows even use the banned native window.confirm(). The kit ships <ConfirmDialog> precisely for this and it is almost entirely unused.
- **Рекомендация:** Gate every destructive/irreversible/consequential action behind the kit <ConfirmDialog> (danger variant) with a named consequence summary (who/what is affected, reversibility). Replace all window.confirm() calls. For reversible bulk actions, pair an undo Toast instead. Embed required justification (e.g. dual-control reject reason) inside the dialog. Visually separate Publish/Deploy from Save.
- **Задача:** `Gate all destructive actions behind kit ConfirmDialog + undo toasts`

### 2. 🔴 Brandbook showcase crashes at runtime, leaving the design system with no source of truth

- **Severity:** critical · **Усилие:** S
- **Охват:** shell-and-design-system — 1 file (showcase.jsx), but it is the consistency reference for all 48 files
- **Проблема:** The design-system showcase imports only React but calls ReactDOM.createRoot, throwing 'ReactDOM is not defined' on load and rendering nothing. The canonical brandbook every screen is meant to inherit from is non-functional, removing any enforceable visual/consistency reference — a root cause of the pervasive drift seen across all areas.
- **Рекомендация:** Add `import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client';` to showcase.jsx, verify it mounts in the dev build, and treat it as the living reference. Then audit the showcase's own internal violations (raw px, ▾ glyph, raw <button>) so the reference itself models correct token/primitive usage.
- **Задача:** `Fix ReactDOM import so design-system showcase renders`

### 3. 🟠 No app-wired toast/feedback system — outcomes silently swallowed, alert()-blocked, or left stale

- **Severity:** high · **Усилие:** L
- **Охват:** all 6 areas — toast convention unused in shell, forms, rights, ai, core-work, observability — ~30+ files
- **Проблема:** There is no ToastProvider/context, so every tree must independently wire useToasts()+ToastViewport and most don't. Consequences recur everywhere: successful saves/creates/approves/publishes give no confirmation; failures surface via blocking native window.alert() (shell audit-export, inbox) or are only console.error'd (assistant thread CRUD, optimistic state diverging from server); success banners stick and go stale after edits; error toasts that do exist auto-dismiss at a fixed 4000ms with no pause-on-hover so errors vanish before they're read. The most consequential confirmation (e.g. clearing the last pending dual-control request) renders into a branch with no viewport at all.
- **Рекомендация:** Add a ToastProvider + useToastContext (or singleton store) with one documented app-root ToastViewport so any component can push without local wiring. Default success to polite/auto-dismiss and error/warning to assertive role=alert with no auto-dismiss (or pause on pointerenter/focus). Replace all window.alert() and console-only failure paths; clear stale success on edit. Roll out as the single success/error surface across every screen.
- **Задача:** `Ship global ToastProvider + viewport and route all outcomes through it`

### 4. 🟠 Controls hand-rolled as raw <input>/<select>/<button> instead of kit <Field>/<Select>/<Button>

- **Severity:** high · **Усилие:** L
- **Охват:** all 6 areas — canvas panels, forms engine, rights, core-work, ai, observability, shell — ~35 files
- **Проблема:** Every area re-rolls native controls with chs-input/chs-btn classes and manual aria wiring instead of the kit primitives the contract mandates, often inconsistently within a single file (some fields use <Field>, siblings are raw). The cost is duplicated label/error/required scaffolding that drifts, lost focus-ring, missing aria-invalid/aria-disabled/aria-busy handling, and broken label↔control binding. field-renderer alone re-implements this across ~10 control types.
- **Рекомендация:** Migrate text/number/date/url/money inputs to <Field>, enums to <Select>, and actions to <Button variant=… glyph=… loading>, removing inline styles so focus-ring/aria/theming come from the kit. Where bpmn-js chrome genuinely forbids it, document the exception per-site and at minimum wire the aria/label bindings the kit would have provided. This single migration collapses the label-binding, focus-ring, and required-marker duplication seen across the audit.
- **Задача:** `Migrate raw form controls to kit Field/Select/Button primitives`

### 5. 🟠 Primary interactive surfaces are keyboard-inaccessible and expose fake/incomplete ARIA

- **Severity:** high · **Усилие:** L
- **Охват:** canvas (BPMN modeler, accordions), forms (drag-drop designer), shell (palette, RoleAssignment), rights/core-work/observability (tabs, trees, clickable rows) — ~20 files
- **Проблема:** Core authoring and navigation surfaces lock out keyboard and screen-reader users (WCAG 2.1.1/4.1.2). The BPMN canvas is instantiated with keyboard.bindTo=null and has no role/label; the form designer's drag-and-drop reorder has no keyboard fallback; properties accordions are non-semantic <div>s with no key handlers; command palette/listbox declares role=option without arrow-key handling or aria-activedescendant; clickable <tr>/<div> rows have no tabIndex/role/onKeyDown; and tabs/trees advertise aria-selected on plain buttons with no tablist/roving-tabindex/arrow-key model — fake ARIA that misleads AT.
- **Рекомендация:** Make every bespoke interactive surface real: bind bpmn-js keyboard to the container + add role=application/aria-label and a visually-hidden diagram summary; add explicit move-up/down controls as the accessible reorder path; convert accordion headers and clickable rows to real <button>/focusable controls with Enter/Space; implement true arrow-key navigation + aria-activedescendant for the palette. For repeated tab/tree patterns, extract shared kit primitives (see design-system gaps) so screens stop re-rolling broken ARIA.
- **Задача:** `Make canvas, designer, palette, rows and tabs keyboard-operable`

### 6. 🟠 Loading/empty/error states hand-rolled instead of kit primitives, often with no role or retry

- **Severity:** high · **Усилие:** L
- **Охват:** all 6 areas — forms, canvas, rights, core-work, observability, shell — ~25 files
- **Проблема:** Async states are bespoke <div>/<p> ('Загрузка…', 'Не удалось загрузить…', bare '—'/'…') with no role=status/aria-busy, no consistent «Повторить», and frequent inline inconsistency. Worse, several failures are swallowed: cross-app-links and grant-trail errors set empty arrays then return null, presenting genuine failure (or stale seed data) as 'empty' with no retry. Empty is routinely conflated with error and with perpetual loading.
- **Рекомендация:** Standardize on kit <LoadingState>(role=status,aria-busy)/<ErrorState onRetry>(role=alert)/<EmptyState>/<Skeleton> everywhere. Lift each fetch into a callable so onRetry re-runs it byte-identically (no duplicated retry logic). Strictly distinguish empty (zero results) from error (never return null on a failure path) and never silently fall back to seed/stale data without an honest banner.
- **Задача:** `Replace bespoke loading/empty/error markup with kit state primitives`

### 7. 🟠 Dev-jargon leaks into the Russian no-code UI (UUIDs, JSON, HTTP codes, enums, ISO timestamps, slugs)

- **Severity:** high · **Усилие:** L
- **Охват:** all 6 areas — rights, core-work, forms, ai, observability, canvas — ~30 files
- **Проблема:** Implementation detail is rendered verbatim to non-technical B2B operators throughout: hand-typed 36-char grant UUIDs and <pre>JSON.stringify</pre> dumps, raw server codes (SUBSTITUTION_WIDENS, 'HTTP 500/401', «Tier-${tier}»), English enums shown to Russian users (draft/published, human/agent/service), BPMN element ids and moddle $types in labels, mcp:// URIs, ISO/PT24H deadlines, ms-precision timestamps, raw field.key/slug placeholders, 'control-plane'/'Floor-2'/'descriptor', and the broken «§9.10» spec refs. This directly betrays the product's no-code promise and fails the G5 honest-text gate.
- **Рекомендация:** Build one shared mapping/formatting layer for the app: map HTTP codes and server enums to plain-Russian copy with next steps (401→«Сессия истекла, войдите снова»); resolve UUIDs/keys/slugs to human labels with «—»/«Система» fallbacks and demote any raw id to a <MonoId>/copy affordance; localize all enums via shared label maps; format dates locale-aware (ru-RU) without ms; strip element ids, moddle types and spec refs from primary labels; remove JSON dumps. Log technical detail to console/telemetry, not the UI.
- **Задача:** `Add shared label/format layer to purge dev-jargon from the UI`

### 8. 🟠 Validation/field errors not programmatically tied to controls, not announced, or suppressed until publish

- **Severity:** high · **Усилие:** M
- **Охват:** forms, canvas, rights, core-work — ~18 files
- **Проблема:** Error text renders as detached sibling spans with no id, controls carry aria-invalid but no aria-describedby, and spans lack role=alert/aria-live, so screen-reader users hear 'invalid' but never the reason and post-submit errors aren't announced. Multi-select groups have orphaned htmlFor and no fieldset/role=group. Separately, required/invalid configs often produce no inline feedback at all and only fail at publish/runtime (blank timer deadline, empty role picker, free-text step/field keys binding to non-existent values).
- **Рекомендация:** Give every error span a stable id, set aria-describedby (prefer aria-errormessage) on the invalid control, and add role=alert/aria-live. Wrap multi-value groups in fieldset/legend (or role=group + aria-labelledby/aria-required). Add touched/blur-tracked inline validation that surfaces required-field gaps before publish, and validate free-text key fields against the catalog/known step keys (offer a picker). Adopting <Field>/<Select> wires most of this for free.
- **Задача:** `Wire field errors via aria-describedby and validate before publish`

### 9. 🟠 Enabled controls that are silent no-ops, and disabled controls whose reason is hidden in a title tooltip

- **Severity:** high · **Усилие:** M
- **Охват:** rights, ai, core-work, observability, shell — ~12 files
- **Проблема:** The affordance rule is violated both ways. Enabled-but-dead: grant-trail «Экспорт» has no onClick, fake «Поиск роли» is a static div, ⌘K overview hint wires to nothing, the assistant-prompt 'promote' step instructs users to POST a raw API with no in-product button. Disabled-without-reason: honest-disabled topbar/submit/publish buttons explain themselves only via native title or a wrapping Tooltip on a native disabled button — invisible to keyboard, touch and AT (the very users who need it).
- **Рекомендация:** Make every enabled control do something or mark it disabled/«скоро» with a keyboard-reachable, visible explanation (inline helper text or aria-describedby, never native title on a disabled element; use aria-disabled to keep focusability). Wire or remove dead actions (Экспорт, fake search, ⌘K, promote). Indicate which required field blocks a disabled submit.
- **Задача:** `Eliminate dead controls and surface disabled-reason accessibly`

### 10. 🟡 Unicode/emoji glyphs used as icons instead of the kit Lucide <Icon> primitive

- **Severity:** medium · **Усилие:** M
- **Охват:** shell, canvas, forms, rights, ai, core-work — ~14 files
- **Проблема:** Literal glyphs are used as icons in violation of the Lucide/currentColor contract: ▾/▴▾ disclosure carets, ★☆✎✕ thread actions, ✗ failure, ●◐○ status, ↑↓ reorder, × remove, ← back, … overflow, ≤/# operators. These ignore icon sizing/stroke/color tokens, render inconsistently cross-platform, are misread by screen readers ('black down-pointing triangle'), and ✕/✗ misread as 'close' rather than 'delete/fail'. The Icon factory also renders a silent blank box for unknown names with no dev warning.
- **Рекомендация:** Replace all glyph-icons with kit <Icon> (chevron-down, star, pencil, trash, x, arrow-up/down, more-horizontal). Use <StatusChip> with text for status rather than shape glyphs. Add a visible fallback + dev-only console.warn for unknown Icon names. Pass icons via the Button glyph prop, preserving aria-labels.
- **Задача:** `Replace Unicode glyph icons with kit Lucide Icon primitive`

### 11. 🟡 Hardcoded colors/hex-fallbacks/px/weights bypass design tokens

- **Severity:** medium · **Усилие:** M
- **Охват:** all 6 areas — canvas, forms, rights, ai, observability, shell — ~25 files
- **Проблема:** Token-contract violations recur widely: raw hex fallbacks inside var() defaults (#c00, #2563eb, #222, green/red) that defeat dark-theme WCAG-AA, raw fontWeight:600, and pixel literals for spacing/sizing/radius — ironically including the showcase and Icon factory whose job is to model token discipline. The opaque-origin form iframe hardcodes #222/system-ui/14px, rendering dark-on-dark in dark theme. Money is formatted with toFixed(4) in the default sans stack instead of mono.
- **Рекомендация:** Consume only semantic --chs-* tokens (danger/warning/accent, --chs-space-*, --chs-weight-semibold, --chs-font-mono); drop every hex/weight/px literal and verify referenced tokens exist. For the iframe, inject the parent's computed --chs-color-* and font tokens as inline CSS variables with an explicit background so dark-theme contrast stays AA. Use Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU',{style:'currency'}) + <Mono> for money. Move recurring inline layout into tokenized utility classes.
- **Задача:** `Replace hardcoded colors/px/weights with semantic design tokens`

### 12. 🟡 Ad-hoc div/span data tables and bespoke section primitives lack semantics and converge nowhere

- **Severity:** medium · **Усилие:** L
- **Охват:** rights (audit log, grant matrix, request rows), observability (notifications/reports matrices), core-work (schema role=table) — ~10 files
- **Проблема:** Tabular data is rendered as CSS-grid divs/spans with no table semantics, so screen readers get a flat cell stream with no column-header association. Some screens add role=table without row/cell roles — worse than none (AT reports a malformed/empty table). Each screen also re-invents SectionHead/Segmented/badges/chips because no shared Card/Table/Tabs/Badge primitives exist, producing two competing tab idioms in a single section.
- **Рекомендация:** Use real <table> with scope (or complete role=table/row/columnheader/cell) for the audit log, grant matrix and preference matrices; drop incomplete role=table where the surface is really a form. Extract shared <DataTable>/<Card>/<Tabs>/<Badge> kit primitives and converge the bespoke per-screen variants onto them.
- **Задача:** `Add table semantics and extract shared Table/Card/Badge primitives`

### 13. 🟡 Meaning carried by color/opacity/icon-count alone with no text equivalent

- **Severity:** medium · **Усилие:** M
- **Охват:** rights, forms, core-work, observability, shell — ~12 files
- **Проблема:** WCAG 1.4.1 use-of-color failures recur: criticality axes as single letters explained only by native title; one-vs-two checkmarks signalling single vs dual sign-off with no accessible name (a lost compliance signal); 8px executor glyphs whose title is the name not the type; field chips dimmed to opacity:0.5 for 'already used'; SLA urgency, inbox row status and the permanent created-row success tint all conveyed by color alone; ThemeToggle selected-state possibly color-only.
- **Рекомендация:** Pair every meaning-bearing visual with a text/AT cue: accessible names on glyphs/icons, a text label or visually-hidden note for dimmed/used/disabled states, role=progressbar+aria-valuetext for the SLA meter, and a non-color affordance (icon/border/text) for status and toggle-selected states. Make the created-row highlight transient and announced via the success toast rather than a permanent tint.
- **Задача:** `Add text/AT equivalents to color- and glyph-only state cues`

### 14. 🟡 Data-loss risks: unsaved diagram/form work discarded and stale/optimistic state diverges from server

- **Severity:** medium · **Усилие:** M
- **Охват:** core-work (process-editor, inbox), ai (prompt editor, connections), observability (reports promote) — ~6 files
- **Проблема:** User effort is silently lost: the BPMN editor tracks isDirty but nothing blocks navigation/tab-close/route change from discarding the diagram; the prompt editor's 'Обновить'/reload overwrites typed-but-unsaved text; provider change overwrites endpoint/model/prices despite an 'only if blank' comment; and reports promote falls through to optimistically mark a row 'published' even when the server rejected it (a dishonest state the user never sees because the builder unmounts).
- **Рекомендация:** Add a beforeunload handler + in-app navigation guard (ConfirmDialog/useBlocker) whenever isDirty. Track a dirty flag and confirm/warn before overwriting edited input; for provider presets only fill empty/unchanged fields. On promote failure, return early and keep the builder open with the real server error — never claim 'published' on a rejected request.
- **Задача:** `Guard unsaved diagram/form edits and fix optimistic publish state`

### 15. 🟡 Overlay/tooltip focus management, dismissal, and live-region correctness gaps

- **Severity:** medium · **Усилие:** M
- **Охват:** shell (Modal/Drawer/Popover/Tooltip), forms (sandboxed iframe submit), ai (assistant listbox/log) — ~5 files
- **Проблема:** Kit overlays mis-handle focus and dismissal: Modal/Drawer close on mousedown so a text-selection drag releasing on the backdrop silently dismisses unsaved input; Popover declares role=dialog but never moves/traps/restores focus and lacks accessible naming and aria-expanded/controls; Tooltip wraps a redundant focusable span with no Esc-to-dismiss. The assistant thread list is an invalid listbox embedding interactive controls, and its message log nests competing aria-live regions causing duplicate announcements; the in-iframe form submit isn't locked during pending (double-submit).
- **Рекомендация:** Switch backdrop dismissal to onClick and gate dirty Modals behind a dirty-check. Bring Popover to dialog parity (focus first focusable, trap+restore, accept labelId, add aria-expanded/controls) or downgrade to a non-dialog menu. Move tooltip aria-describedby onto the already-interactive child + add Esc. Model the thread list as a plain navigable list, keep a single live region for the message log, and postMessage a 'submitting' signal to disable the in-iframe submit.
- **Задача:** `Fix overlay focus/dismissal and live-region correctness in the kit`

---

## Пробелы дизайн-системы

_Отсутствующие примитивы/конвенции — закрытие каждого чинит целый класс проблем:_

- No global Toast/feedback provider: ToastProvider + context/singleton store with one app-root ToastViewport is missing, forcing every tree to wire useToasts() locally — the root cause of swallowed outcomes, window.alert() fallbacks, and stale inline success banners across all areas.
- No shared <Tabs>/<Segmented> primitive: every tab/sub-tab/filter strip (rights RightsSubTabs, forms, inbox, notifications, ra-shell, grant-trail) re-rolls aria-selected on plain buttons with no tablist/tab/tabpanel, roving tabindex, or arrow-key model — broken ARIA duplicated 6+ times.
- No shared <Table>/<DataTable> primitive: audit log, grant matrix, request rows, notification/report matrices are hand-assembled CSS-grid divs with no table/row/columnheader/cell semantics or scope.
- No <Tree>/<TreeItem> primitive: the org tree hand-rolls expand/select with no tree/treeitem roles, nests interactive controls inside a native button (invalid HTML), and is mouse-only.
- No <Checkbox> primitive: checkboxes are raw <input type=checkbox> with duplicated inline styling, missing focus-ring tokens, and nesting-only label association (canvas OutcomeRow, forms multi-select, notifications matrix).
- No <Textarea>/multiline or code-input primitive: textareas are raw across forms, rights (SoD scope/LLM), ai (system prompt, composer), losing label binding and consistent styling.
- No searchable <Select>/combobox primitive: relation/trigger/process pickers and the audit actor filter fall back to raw selects or free-text identifier fields with no typeahead.
- No <Card>/<Panel> surface primitive: card chrome, tiles and raised sections are assembled from per-file inline-style objects, inviting border/padding/casing drift across the observability and rights surfaces.
- No <MonoId>/identifier-display convention: raw UUIDs, slugs and keys are printed as primary content with no canonical copy-on-click muted-mono component to demote them.
- No shared label/error/required scaffolding contract enforced: <Field>/<Select> exist but are bypassed, so aria-describedby error binding, the required '*' marker, and focus-ring are re-implemented (and drift) per control — they should be the only sanctioned path with a documented bpmn-js exception.
- No app-wide error-code → human-copy mapping utility and no shared ru-RU date/money formatter: each screen maps HTTP/enum/timestamp/currency ad hoc (or not at all), causing dev-jargon leaks and toFixed(4) money.
- Icon factory has no unknown-name fallback or dev warning (silent blank box), no token-driven sizing (hardcoded 16x16/strokeWidth), and no path to an accessible name for icon-only controls.
- No documented dirty-state/navigation-guard helper: unsaved-edit protection (beforeunload + in-app blocker) is absent, so diagram/form work is silently discarded.
- Toast auto-dismiss timing has no pause-on-hover/focus and no error-stays-open default, so role=alert errors can vanish before being read.

## Quick wins (низкое усилие, высокий эффект)

- Add the missing `import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client'` to showcase.jsx so the brandbook reference renders (S, critical, 1-line).
- Add type="button" to every hand-rolled <button> in shell, showcase, forms and rights to clear the latent submit footgun.
- Replace Unicode glyph icons (▾ ★☆✎✕ ✗ ↑↓ × ← …) with kit <Icon> equivalents, preserving existing aria-labels.
- Replace all window.alert() and window.confirm() calls (shell audit-export, inbox, ra-sod) with kit Toast / ConfirmDialog.
- Convert properties-panel accordion header <div role=button> and clickable <tr>/<div> rows to real <button>/focusable controls with Enter/Space — unlocks keyboard operability with minimal code.
- Wire ra-criticality's dual-control reject to pass the already-supported `reason` (currently hardcoded null) so denials carry justification into the audit log.
- Add an unknown-name fallback glyph + dev console.warn to the Icon factory so missing icons aren't silent blank boxes.
- Move disabled-control reasons out of native title/Tooltip-on-disabled into visible inline helper text or aria-describedby (topbar buttons, publish, submit gating).
- Drop hex/green/red literal fallbacks inside var() defaults and consume the defined semantic --chs-color-* tokens (one mechanical sweep).
- Make the created-row success tint transient (fade after ~2-3s, honoring reduced-motion) instead of a permanent color-only status.
- Add fieldset/legend (or role=group + aria-labelledby) to the multi-select checkbox group and gateway default-radio group, and remove the orphaned htmlFor.
- Hoist duplicated load-vs-retry fetch logic (login loadUsers, FormBuilder apps/defs) into a single useCallback so retry behaves identically to initial load.

---

## Темы по областям

### ai-and-automation (14 тем)

- 🟠 **Action outcomes (save/create/bind/test) not announced to screen readers — missing aria-live roles** _(M)_
  - Across nearly every screen in this area, post-action feedback (submit errors, save/create/publish success, key-bind results, connection-test results, auto-save hint cycling) renders in plain styled <div>/<span> elements with no role=alert (assertive) for errors or role=status/aria-live=polite for success/progress. Screen-reader and keyboard users who trigger an action get no notification of whether it succeeded or failed, and silently-reverting failures (e.g. the auto-save LLM-connection select snapping back) are especially opaque. The automated AA checks missed these because the live-region attributes simply aren't present to evaluate. This is a recurring WCAG 4.1.3 (Status Messages, AA) gap and directly violates the contract's Error=role=alert / success=role=status convention.
  - → Adopt one consistent feedback path: route transient outcomes through the kit useToasts()+<ToastViewport> (error=alert/assertive, success=status/polite). Where inline banners remain, add role="alert" to error banners and role="status"+aria-live="polite" to success/progress banners, and wrap auto-save progress/success/error hints in a live region so silently-reverting failures are spoken.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-agents.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-config.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-connections.jsx`
- 🟠 **Destructive / irreversible actions fire immediately with no confirmation or undo** _(M)_
  - Multiple consequential actions execute on a single click with no <ConfirmDialog> and no undo: unbinding an encrypted API key (irreversible, breaks dependent agents), deleting a branch-routing rule with all its conditions, clearing a long hand-authored system prompt, and re-binding a running agent's LLM connection on a stray change event. The contract ships <ConfirmDialog> precisely for these and bans bare window.confirm-style or no-confirm destructive flows.
  - → Gate genuinely destructive/irreversible actions (key unbind, non-empty rule delete, prompt clear) behind <ConfirmDialog> with a clear consequence message, or offer an undo toast via useToasts(). For consequential-but-reversible changes like re-binding an agent's LLM, pair inline-save with a clear confirmation toast (or a ConfirmDialog when an already-bound agent moves to a different/empty connection). Skip the prompt only for trivially-empty rows.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-llm-connections.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-agents.jsx`
- 🟠 **Silent failures and stale/persistent success feedback violate the explicit-states + transient-outcome conventions** _(M)_
  - Two opposite anti-patterns recur. (1) Silent no-ops: thread create/rename/pin/delete failures are only console.error'd (optimistic state diverges from server with no UI signal), and the routing editor silently filters out half-filled rules then reports '201 success' — false-positive success with the incomplete rule discarded. (2) Sticky success: save/create/publish confirmations render as persistent inline banners that never auto-dismiss and aren't cleared when the user edits, so 'Черновик сохранён' / 'Профиль создан.' lingers and contradicts current state. The contract designates Toast for transient outcomes and requires every action to either succeed visibly or surface its error.
  - → Make outcomes transient via useToasts() (auto-dismissing toasts) and surface every failure: await mutations and show an error toast (rolling back optimistic rename/pin state on failure). Clear/dirty stale success the moment any field changes. Add pre-submit validation in the routing editor (each non-default rule needs a complete condition and non-empty outcome; value required for value-requiring operators) and block submit with per-field errors instead of silently filtering.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-config.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-connections.jsx`
- 🟠 **Emoji / Unicode glyphs used as icons instead of the kit Lucide <Icon> primitive** _(S)_
  - Screens render literal Unicode characters as icons in violation of the contract's 'no emoji/✓✗ as icons; linear Lucide currentColor via <Icon>' rule: thread pin/rename/delete buttons use ★ ☆ ✎ ✕, the connection-tester failure state uses a raw ✗ (while success correctly uses <Icon name="check"/>), the prompt-editor disclosure uses ▲/▼ arrows, and tier status uses ●/◐/○ (the half-circle ◐ has no defined meaning and collides with the executor-glyph vocabulary). These glyphs ignore icon sizing/stroke/color tokens, render inconsistently across platforms, and ✕/✗ misread as 'close' rather than 'delete/fail'.
  - → Replace all glyph-icons with kit <Icon> (pin/edit/trash, x for failure, ChevronDown/ChevronUp for disclosure). For status, use <StatusChip> with a text meaning-bearer rather than decorative shape glyphs, and never reuse the ●/◆/■ executor-glyph vocabulary for unrelated status.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-connections.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`
- 🟠 **Promote step described but has no in-product affordance — system-prompt workflow dead-ends** _(M)_
  - The assistant-prompt editor lets users save a draft, then repeatedly tells them it only takes effect after 'promote' — but provides no button or link to promote, instructing users to call POST /api/artifacts/promote themselves. The core job (make a new prompt live) cannot be completed on-screen, so every successful save dead-ends and the success toast points at an API the user cannot invoke. Violates the affordance principle that an enabled flow must lead somewhere.
  - → Add an 'Опубликовать'/promote action (gated by ConfirmDialog) that calls the promote endpoint and reflects the new published tier; or, if promote is intentionally out of scope, mark it «скоро»/handled-elsewhere with a link instead of instructing users to hit a raw API.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`
- 🟡 **Dev-jargon leaks — raw slugs, internal field/variable names, endpoint paths and HTTP codes shown in UI** _(M)_
  - The contract bans dev-jargon, code-names, UUIDs and identifier leaks in the UI, yet B2B-operator-facing copy exposes plumbing across the area: raw agent slugs as primary identifiers; raw processKey and a camelCase 'approvalRequired' variable in the routing editor; '/api/artifacts/promote', '(тип: agent_instruction)', and bare 'HTTP 401/409/${status}' in the prompt editor; '(503)', 'HTTP 404/{status}', 'agent_card' and 'тенант' in the LLM-config screen. These are meaningless to business users and read as leaked internals.
  - → Replace endpoint/type/variable references with user-facing language and human identifiers (show display names/positions, not slugs; map provider variables to human labels). Map HTTP codes to plain-language causes ('Сессия истекла, войдите снова', 'Промпт уже опубликован', 'Ассистент недоступен, пока не настроен провайдер'), keeping any raw code only in secondary diagnostic detail. Rephrase tenant isolation as «настройки видны только вашей организации».
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-agents.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-config.jsx`
- 🟡 **App-content controls hand-rolled instead of kit primitives (Button / Field / StatusChip / Card / banners)** _(L)_
  - Screens reinvent design-system primitives with bespoke inline-styled elements, causing affordance drift and lost token behavior (focus-ring, hover/active, aria-busy/disabled, sizing). Recurring instances: raw <button> for app-content actions (thread rows/actions, composer 'убрать', rule delete/condition-remove/add, back-nav); raw <input>/<textarea> without <Field> label binding; hand-rolled status badges/dots instead of <StatusChip>; ad-hoc <section>/banner divs instead of a <Card>/<Panel>/feedback primitive (with three separate bespoke banner systems on the connections screen alone); a bare <input type=checkbox>; and an unused EmptyState import. Only shell nav buttons get the bespoke exception.
  - → Migrate app-content actions to <Button variant="ghost"/secondary glyph={<Icon/>}>, wrap text inputs in <Field> (label↔control binding + aria-describedby hints), render status via <StatusChip>, and consolidate success/error feedback on the kit Toast/feedback primitives instead of per-screen banner style objects. Where the kit lacks a wrapper (textarea, checkbox, Card/Panel), flag/propose the primitive rather than re-declaring styles per screen; remove dead imports.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-config.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-connections.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`
- 🟡 **Form labels not programmatically bound to their controls (htmlFor/id missing)** _(S)_
  - Text controls rely solely on aria-label rather than a real label↔control binding: the system-prompt <textarea> has a visible label with no htmlFor/matching id (clicking the label doesn't focus, and the visible text diverges from the aria-label string), and the rename <input> and composer <textarea> on the assistant screen are raw elements with no <Field> wrapper. This is a WCAG 1.3.1 / 3.3.2 labels-on-inputs gap that the kit's Field/Select primitives exist to handle.
  - → Use the <Field> primitive (or add an id to the control and htmlFor on the label) so the visible label becomes the accessible name; drop redundant aria-labels or make them match the visible text. Surface field hints (e.g. char limits, Enter/Shift+Enter affordance) via aria-describedby rather than placeholder-only.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`
- 🟡 **Invalid ARIA listbox composition and nested/competing live regions in the assistant thread list & message log** _(M)_
  - Single-screen but structurally significant on the assistant screen: the thread list is role=listbox with role=option buttons but also embeds interactive pin/rename/delete controls and lacks roving-tabindex/arrow-key handling, so arrow-key listbox navigation can't reach the actions and the 'listbox' doesn't behave like one. Separately, the messages container (role=log aria-live=polite) nests another aria-live=polite streaming-dots bubble, causing duplicate/competing announcements, while success states ('Отчёт сохранён', 'Решение опубликовано') are plain spans with no role=status — inconsistent with the role=alert errors.
  - → Model the thread list as a plain navigable list (items as link/button, actions as normal focusable controls) rather than a listbox, given it doubles as router navigation. Keep a single live region for messages (let role=log handle new messages; remove aria-live from the streaming dots) and wrap success spans in role=status to mirror the error role=alert.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`
- ⚪ **Disabled-state reason hidden in title tooltip — not perceivable to keyboard/touch/AT users** _(S)_
  - Disabled controls explain their gating only via a native title attribute, which is invisible on touch, unreliable on disabled elements across browsers, and skipped by some assistive tech: the routing-editor Publish button ('Сначала сохраните черновик'), and the assistant 'Сохранить как отчёт' button ('Откройте ассистента из приложения…'). The contract's affordance rule requires disabled state to be understandable.
  - → Render the gating reason as visible inline helper text near the action group whenever the control is disabled (in addition to or instead of the title attribute), so the reason is always perceivable.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`
- ⚪ **Disclosure widgets missing aria-expanded / aria-controls linkage** _(S)_
  - Expand/collapse disclosures don't fully expose state and relationship to AT: the agents activity toggle sets aria-expanded but the panel has no id and the button no aria-controls (and the panel isn't focus-managed), and the prompt-editor default-prompt disclosure (a raw button) lacks aria-expanded entirely plus an aria-controls link to its <pre> region.
  - → Give each disclosed region a stable id and point aria-controls={id} from the trigger; ensure the trigger carries aria-expanded reflecting open state. Optionally mark the region role="region" with an aria-label so it is identifiable when expanded.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-agents.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`
- ⚪ **Hardcoded px / weight / radius / font values bypass design tokens** _(S)_
  - Several screens use literal values instead of --chs-* tokens, which the contract treats as violations: fontWeight:600 and letterSpacing:'0.04em' in the routing editor; raw 8px dot dimensions, '50%'/'999px' radii, '2px 6px'/'2px 8px' paddings, a raw 820px max-width, and 'monospace' font fallbacks in the LLM config/connections screens.
  - → Replace literals with the corresponding tokens (--chs-weight-semibold, tracking tokens, --chs-space-*, --chs-radius-full/pill, var(--chs-font-mono)) or move them into shared CSS classes; introduce a layout token for max-width if one doesn't exist.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-config.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-connections.jsx`
- ⚪ **Unsaved / user-edited input silently overwritten without a dirty-state guard** _(S)_
  - User work is discarded with no warning: 'Обновить' (and post-save reload) in the prompt editor overwrites typed-but-unsaved text, and changing the provider in the connections form unconditionally overwrites endpoint/model/prices (contradicting the code comment's 'only if blank' intent). Neither shows a dirty indicator or confirms before replacing.
  - → Track a dirty flag (current value !== server/preset value) and disable, confirm, or warn before overwriting; for provider presets, only fill fields that are empty or still equal the previous preset default, preserving user edits otherwise.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-connections.jsx`
- ⚪ **One-shot success states and conditional fields dead-end without follow-through or explanation** _(M)_
  - Minor flow/affordance gaps: after save-as-report/publish the assistant swaps the button for a static label with no link to the created report/published solution and no re-entry; in the routing editor the «Значение» field vanishes (replaced by an empty aria-hidden div with an orphan grid gap) for present/absent operators with no microcopy; the LLM-config 'Обновить статус' ghost button duplicates implicit refresh with no explanation of when to use it; and disclosure <pre>/textarea render bare emptiness when default_text is missing instead of an explicit Empty state.
  - → Turn success confirmations into actionable deep-links ('— открыть'); render a muted hint ('значение не требуется') or collapse the column when the value field is hidden; either remove the redundant refresh button or annotate when to use it ('после привязки ключа на экране Агенты'); and fall back to <EmptyState>/a literal placeholder when default content is absent.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-assistant.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-dmn-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-llm-config.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx`

### auth-and-onboarding (7 тем)

- 🟠 **Submit/redirect actions lack inline loading-busy state (kit Button loading prop unused)** _(S)_
  - Primary auth actions don't surface in-flight feedback the way the kit already supports. On login, the keycloak "Войти" button fires a full-page OIDC redirect with no loading/aria-busy state, so on a cold Keycloak the user sees nothing happen and may double-click or assume breakage. On register, the busy state is handled by swapping the entire form out for a standalone centered LoadingState, causing a form→spinner→form flash and layout jump on failure rather than keeping context. Both screens have a Button with an available `loading` prop that would give spinner + aria-busy in place.
  - → Standardize on the kit Button's `loading`/`disabled` props for the primary action. On login, track `submitting`, set on click, render <Button loading disabled> to prevent double-submit during redirect. On register, keep the form mounted with fields disabled and the Button in loading state instead of swapping to a full-screen LoadingState, preserving entered values and context.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-login.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-register.jsx`
- 🟡 **Error feedback on submit is incomplete: no focus management and missing standard Retry affordance** _(S)_
  - Single-file (register) but high-impact gaps in the post-submit error path. The ErrorState renders with role=alert (announces once) but focus is never moved to the error container or first invalid field and it does not scroll into view, so after the loading swap a keyboard/SR user is left at the form with no guidance (WCAG 3.3.1). Separately, network failures (status 0) map to a generic message and the ErrorState is rendered without onRetry, so the standard app-wide "Повторить" affordance is absent — the only recovery is re-pressing the primary button, despite the copy telling the user to retry.
  - → After setSubmitError, move focus to the error container (ref + tabIndex=-1 .focus()) or the first invalid field and scrollIntoView, keeping role=alert for the announcement. Pass onRetry={handleSubmit} to ErrorState so the standard Повторить button appears, and give status 0 (network/offline) distinct copy from a generic server error.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-register.jsx`
- 🟡 **Content buttons hand-rolled instead of using the kit, with no focus-visible ring** _(M)_
  - The dev-mode identity-picker rows on login are raw <button> elements with bespoke CSS, even though selecting an identity is the primary action of the screen and the design contract requires content actions to consume the kit <Button>. The CSS defines :hover and :active but no :focus-visible (the sibling register link does), so a keyboard user tabbing the identity list gets only the inconsistent UA outline, ambiguous against the accent :active styling — a real WCAG 2.4.7 gap on a login screen. The same buttons also lack an explicit type attribute, so a bare <button> defaults to type=submit and would trigger an unintended submit if ever nested in a <form>.
  - → Refactor each identity row to a kit primitive that owns the focus ring (raising the gap if no list-row Button variant exists). As an interim, add `.chs-login-user-btn:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--chs-color-focus-ring); outline-offset: 2px; }` mirroring the register link, and set type="button" on the native button.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-login.jsx`
- ⚪ **Hand-rolled client-side navigation links bypass the app's routing primitive** _(M)_
  - Both auth screens reimplement routing inline for their cross-links. The login "Создать организацию" link and the register "Войти" link manually call preventDefault/history.pushState and dispatch a synthetic PopStateEvent to drive a re-render. This bespoke shim is fragile (any change to how the shell listens for route changes breaks it silently) and inconsistent with the rest of the app. The login link's unconditional preventDefault also hijacks cmd/ctrl/middle-click into same-tab nav, breaking open-in-new-tab.
  - → Route both links through the app's shared navigate(path) helper (the same one the shell uses); extract one if none exists so the pattern is defined once. At minimum, guard handlers to bail on modified clicks (metaKey/ctrlKey/shiftKey || button !== 0) so open-in-new-tab keeps working, and confirm href targets match the real routes.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-login.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-register.jsx`
- ⚪ **Form validation messaging and timing polish** _(S)_
  - Single-file (register) validation polish. The persistent password constraint hint ('Не менее 8 символов') shares the same slot the validation error overwrites, and the two are near-duplicate phrasings of the same rule, so the requirement appears to flicker between two wordings as the user types. Separately, validation runs only on submit, so a mistyped email gives no signal until the primary button is pressed; blur-time validation after the first failed submit is the standard pattern and removes a round-trip.
  - → Keep the constraint hint always visible and visually distinct from the error (or align both to identical wording, e.g. 'Минимум 8 символов'). After the first submit attempt, validate the touched field on blur while preserving clear-on-change to avoid nagging mid-typing.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-register.jsx`
- ⚪ **Duplicated fetch logic on the load-vs-retry error path** _(S)_
  - Single-file (login) consistency liability. The initial /api/users load (in useEffect) and the handleRetry path are byte-for-byte copies of the same fetch + error-mapping logic. They can drift so that a future tweak to error copy or endpoint lands on only one path, producing inconsistent loading/error/empty behavior between first load and retry. Not user-visible today but a UX-consistency risk on the error path.
  - → Hoist a single loadUsers callback (useCallback) and call it from both useEffect and handleRetry so loading/error/empty behavior is guaranteed identical between first load and retry.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-login.jsx`
- ⚪ **Dev-environment jargon leaks into user-facing copy** _(S)_
  - Single-file (login) content-clarity issue. The dev-mode subtitle reads 'вход (dev)', surfacing the environment/mode name to a person, which the design contract bars (no dev-jargon in UI). It is the mildest form (mode-signalling, not a code-name/UUID) and only appears in the dev auth branch, but it is still developer terminology shown in the product.
  - → Confirm the keycloak branch is the only production path. If there is any chance the dev branch reaches non-developers, drop the '(dev)' suffix or use neutral copy (e.g. 'тестовый вход').
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-login.jsx`

### core-work-screens (19 тем)

- 🟠 **No success confirmation after create/save/approve actions** _(M)_
  - Across the constructor and inbox surfaces, successful mutations (record created, schema saved, application created, claim/approve in inbox) give no explicit transient confirmation. The kit ships useToasts()/<Toast>/<ToastViewport> for exactly this, but screens instead close a drawer/modal silently, re-fetch a list, or rely on a row tint the user may never see if the new row sorts off-screen. The user is left guessing whether the save succeeded — acute for irreversible inbox approvals.
  - → Wire useToasts()+<ToastViewport> once into each screen tree and fire a status (polite) toast on every successful 200/201/approve (e.g. «Запись создана», «Поля сохранены», «Приложение создано», «Шаг согласован», «Задача взята»). Standardize this as the single success-feedback surface instead of silent close + tint.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`
- 🟠 **Inline/field validation errors not programmatically associated with their controls** _(M)_
  - Bespoke form fields (relation pickers, collection/line-items, slug/name/description, schema field rows, trigger-binding selects) set aria-invalid on the control but render the error message as a detached sibling <span> with no id, no aria-describedby link, and no role=alert/aria-live. Screen-reader users hear 'invalid' but never the reason, and newly-appearing errors are not announced. This diverges from the kit <Field>/<Select>, which wire label↔control↔error via aria-describedby automatically.
  - → Route these custom fields through the kit <Field>/<Select> wrappers so error binding is automatic. Where a bespoke wrapper is unavoidable, give each error span a stable id, reference it from the control via aria-describedby (prefer aria-errormessage), and add aria-live=polite/role=alert so the message is announced as it appears.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-processes.jsx`
- 🟠 **Raw <input>/<select>/<textarea> bypass kit <Field>/<Select> primitives** _(L)_
  - Multiple forms hand-roll raw <input>/<select>/<textarea> with chs-input classes and manual aria-label/aria-invalid wiring instead of the kit <Field>/<Select> primitives the contract requires — and often inconsistently within one file (e.g. some fields use <Field> while siblings are raw). This loses centralized label binding, focus-ring, aria-invalid and hint plumbing, invites styling drift (manual chs-input--invalid vs kit invalid prop), and surfaces a real kit gap: there is no searchable-select and no <Textarea>/code-input primitive.
  - → Migrate field-set/type/relation/trigger pickers and text inputs to <Select>/<Field> for one source of truth on label binding, focus ring and invalid styling. Where the kit genuinely lacks a primitive (searchable select, multiline textarea, code input), add it to the kit rather than reinventing per-screen.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-processes.jsx`
- 🟠 **Destructive/irreversible actions lack confirmation or undo** _(M)_
  - Consequential actions commit on a single click with no <ConfirmDialog> and no undo: removing a field/column from an existing registry_def (queues data loss surfacing as a server 409), inline «Согласовать» approval that advances/closes a process instance from a dense table, «Опубликовать» that deploys to the live Flowable engine (sitting right next to «Сохранить»), and per-row line-item delete that drops entered data. The kit ships <ConfirmDialog> precisely for these moments.
  - → Gate genuinely destructive/irreversible actions behind <ConfirmDialog> summarizing the consequence (edit-time field removal, inline approve, publish-over-published, deploy version N). For trivial pre-submit row removal with data, offer a lightweight inline undo instead. Visually separate Publish from Save to reduce mis-clicks. Never use window.confirm.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`
- 🟠 **Incomplete/hand-rolled ARIA tabs pattern (no tablist/tab/tabpanel roles, no keyboard model)** _(M)_
  - Tab/filter strips use aria-selected on bespoke buttons but omit the rest of the WAI-ARIA Tabs contract: no role=tablist container, no role=tab + roving tabindex, no arrow-key navigation, and no role=tabpanel/aria-controls linking content. Screen readers announce plain buttons with an unexpected 'selected' state and keyboard users get Tab-through-each instead of arrow navigation. The contract notes no <Tabs> primitive exists, so each strip re-hand-rolls it.
  - → Extract a shared <Tabs>/<SubTabs> primitive (closest prior art is the shell's RightsSubTabs) that owns roles, roving tabindex, arrow/Home/End keys and tabpanel wiring, and adopt it across forms and inbox. For inbox's executor filter, model it as a single-select/radiogroup since these are filter toggles, not panels.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-forms.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`
- 🟠 **Clickable table rows are mouse-only — no keyboard reach or focus** _(S)_
  - Records and applications tables put cursor:pointer + onClick navigate on a bare <tr> with no tabIndex/role/onKeyDown, so keyboard and screen-reader users cannot trigger the primary row navigation; the row's clickability is also invisible to AT. The row click further competes with text selection and with nested relation links (clicking a relation navigates elsewhere than the row), and on records it merely duplicates the already-accessible «Открыть» link.
  - → Make a real focusable control the authoritative navigation affordance — the application name/«Открыть» as a Link/Button to the destination — and drop the <tr> onClick or keep it as a pure pointer convenience. stopPropagation on nested links so inner targets win. If a clickable row is required, add role=button, tabIndex=0, aria-label and Enter/Space onKeyDown.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`
- 🟠 **Loading/error/empty states hand-rolled instead of kit state primitives, often with no role or retry** _(M)_
  - Across records, processes and record-detail, loading/error/empty branches are bespoke styled <div>/<p> (e.g. «Загрузка…», «Не удалось загрузить…», bare «…»/«—») instead of <LoadingState>(role=status,aria-busy)/<ErrorState onRetry>(role=alert)/<EmptyState>/<Skeleton>. AT is not notified of async outcomes, there is no consistent retry affordance, and it is internally inconsistent (the same screen's empty state often correctly uses EmptyState). Worst case in record-detail: a cross-app-links fetch error is fully swallowed (catch sets links=[] then the empty guard returns null), so a genuine failure shows nothing and cannot be retried.
  - → Swap bespoke branches for <LoadingState>/<ErrorState onRetry={load}>/<EmptyState>/<Skeleton>. Distinguish empty (ok, zero results → null/EmptyState) from error (render ErrorState with retry); never return null on an error path. Give compact inline loading regions role=status/aria-busy.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-processes.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-record-detail.jsx`
- 🟠 **Status/outcome banners and meters not announced to screen readers** _(M)_
  - Outcome and status surfaces convey information without programmatic announcement. The process-editor's save/publish/validation banners render in plain <div>s (no role=status/alert, aria-live), and they auto-dismiss on a timer so non-sighted users may never perceive them. The inbox SLA meter is a decorative <span> with no role=progressbar/aria-valuetext, and both the SLA urgency and the row status marker are conveyed by color alone (WCAG 1.4.1). This is a recurring 4.1.3 Status Messages / use-of-color gap on the densest work screens.
  - → Add role=alert/aria-live=assertive to danger outcomes and role=status/aria-live=polite to success/warning (or route through kit <Toast>, which wires roles). Give the SLA meter role=progressbar with aria-valuemin/max/now and aria-valuetext, and pair every status/urgency color with a non-color cue (icon/text/StatusChip/aria-label).
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-process-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`
- 🟠 **Dev-jargon leaks into UI: raw HTTP codes, UUIDs/keys, English enums, ISO timestamps, slug/JSON** _(M)_
  - The contract bans dev-jargon in the UI, yet many screens leak it: raw 'HTTP 500' in error strings; bare UUIDs as primary content and in link tooltips (record-detail header id, created_by author, relation title); raw process_key/instance id and unformatted timestamps risking 1970/ISO in process tables; YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM dev date format; English enum values shown to Russian users ('draft'/'published' chips, 'Тип: agent/human/service'); 'Слаг' label with field_key/my-registry mono placeholders; and object values dumped via JSON.stringify. These read as machine output to no-code business operators.
  - → Map HTTP status codes to human Russian messages before display (reuse the existing mapRecordError approach). Resolve UUIDs/keys/created_by to human labels with «—»/«Система» fallback and never surface raw ids in tooltips. Localize enum values (draft→Черновик, human→Человек, etc.) via shared label maps. Format dates with a shared Russian-locale Intl formatter. Rename/auto-derive 'Слаг' and render structured values readably.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-processes.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-record-detail.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-editor.jsx`
- 🟠 **Errors surfaced via window.alert() and lost/dead feedback instead of the kit feedback queue** _(M)_
  - Failure and integrity feedback bypasses or loses the kit's Toast/state surfaces. Inbox claim/approve failures use blocking window.alert(e.message) — explicitly flagged as a convention violation. The process-editor hand-rolls its own StatusBanner queue (bespoke setTimeout, raw close button, single-message overwrite that drops save-then-validate feedback) and auto-dismisses long actionable error lists in 8s with no history. The schema editor's data-integrity warning banner is dead code: it sets warnings then immediately unmounts the editor via onSaved, so the 'dependent reports affected' warning never reaches the user.
  - → Replace window.alert and the bespoke StatusBanner with useToasts()+<ToastViewport>, configuring error toasts as assertive and non-auto-dismissing (manual close) while success toasts auto-dismiss. Pass schema-save warnings up through onSaved and surface them as a persistent toast/inline banner on the list, or keep the editor open until acknowledged.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`
- 🟡 **Required-field indication is visual-only with no programmatic 'required'** _(S)_
  - Required state is conveyed only by an aria-hidden «*» or by an unlabeled checkbox column whose 'Обяз.' header scrolls away, with no aria-required/required on the actual controls (collection groups, line-item columns, schema field rows) and no on-screen legend decoding the glyph. Assistive-tech users never learn a field/column is mandatory, and sighted users lose the cue in dense scrolling editors.
  - → Pair the visual asterisk with a programmatic signal: add aria-required/required to the controls and collection groups, or append visually-hidden «обязательное поле» text to labels. Show the «* — обязательное» legend once per form, and keep a sticky/per-row label for the required checkbox column.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`
- 🟡 **role=table on a div without row/cell roles (broken ARIA table)** _(S)_
  - The schema field editor wraps rows in role="table" but the header and FieldRow children are role="group", not role=row/columnheader/cell. An incomplete table role is worse than none: AT announces a table then finds no rows/cells and reports it as empty/malformed. The component's own comments argue the editor is a form, not a data table.
  - → Drop role="table" and rely on the visible column header plus per-control aria-labels (simplest, matches the 'this is a form' intent), or fully complete the ARIA contract with role=row/columnheader/cell.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`
- 🟡 **Unsaved BPMN diagram changes are silently discarded on navigation/unload** _(S)_
  - The process editor tracks isDirty and shows a «несохранённые изменения» label, but nothing prevents loss: navigating to the branch-rules editor, route changes, or a browser tab close/refresh silently discards modeler work. For a no-code modeling tool where a diagram represents real effort, this is a significant data-loss risk.
  - → Add a beforeunload handler when isDirty and guard in-app navigation (branch-rules link and router transitions) with a ConfirmDialog/useBlocker prompt when there are unsaved changes.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-process-editor.jsx`
- ⚪ **Disabled primary CTAs explain their gating reason only via title tooltip** _(S)_
  - Disabled primary actions («Создать запись» until a field-set is picked, «Настроить триггер» until defs+apps exist) communicate the blocking reason only through a title attribute, which is mouse-only and not announced to keyboard/touch/screen-reader users. Related empty states describe the situation but offer no inline CTA to resolve it, violating the affordance rule that an empty/blocked state should point to the next step.
  - → Surface the gating reason as visible inline microcopy next to the button (not only title), and give the corresponding EmptyState an actionable control or focus the relevant picker so the blocked state becomes a true next-step.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-processes.jsx`
- ⚪ **Ad-hoc glyphs and inline styles instead of KitIcon and tokens/classes** _(M)_
  - Several screens use literal Unicode characters as icons (↑/↓ reorder, ← back, … overflow/loading, SLA ↑ sort, mirrored chevron for undo/redo) instead of Lucide-style KitIcon via currentColor, rendering at inconsistent weight/baseline. Layout, transforms and even color fallbacks are also encoded as inline style objects with hardcoded values (minHeight:'64px', fontWeight:'500', borderTop with a dark-hex var() fallback, transform/inset one-offs), splitting the styling source of truth and bypassing the token-only rule.
  - → Replace glyph 'icons' with KitIcon (arrow-up/down, arrow-left, more-horizontal, undo-2/redo-2), keeping existing aria-labels. Move layout/transform/border declarations into CSS classes consuming --chs-space/--chs-weight/--chs-color-* tokens, and drop hardcoded hex var() fallbacks (use var(--chs-color-border) etc.).
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-schema.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-processes.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-record-detail.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-forms.jsx`
- ⚪ **Created-row highlight is a permanent, color-only status tint** _(S)_
  - On records and applications, a newly-created row is tinted with --chs-color-success-soft via a highlightId that is never cleared, so a semantic status color persists for the life of the list and overloads scannability when several items are created. The tint is also the only cue (color-only, WCAG 1.4.1) and is meaningless to screen readers.
  - → Make the highlight transient (fade out after ~2-3s honoring reduced-motion, cleaned up on unmount), pair it with the success toast (covering the SR/announcement need) and optionally scroll the new row into view, so it reads as a momentary 'just added' cue rather than a persistent status.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-app-records.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`
- ⚪ **Two-step 'collect then commit' form flow in the inbox drawer is non-obvious** _(S)_
  - In the inbox detail drawer the user first submits the bound form via «Готово» (which only stashes values and shows a notice), then must press a separate footer «Выполнить шаг» to commit. «Готово» looks terminal but does nothing visible, so a user may stop believing the task is done, and there is no way to re-edit the form once «заполнена».
  - → Merge the actions so «Выполнить шаг» reads and validates the form directly, or relabel «Готово» to a clearly intermediate «Сохранить данные формы», ensure required-field validation still runs at commit, and allow re-opening the form.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-inbox.jsx`
- ⚪ **'Открыть' row action navigates to a generic /audit page without instance context** _(S)_
  - Each process-instance row's «Открыть» button routes to '/audit' with no instance id, so the user expecting to drill into that specific instance lands on a generic audit screen. The label promises a destination the route does not deliver, weakening trust in the action.
  - → Pass the instance id to the destination (e.g. /audit?instance={id} or a dedicated /processes/{id} detail) so «Открыть» opens the specific instance, or relabel to match the actual destination.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-processes.jsx`
- ⚪ **401 UNAUTHENTICATED offers no re-authenticate recovery path** _(S)_
  - Despite a documented '401 — войти' intent, the apps screen on 401 only shows a generic submit-error message or a list ErrorState whose «Повторить» will just re-fail for an expired session, with no actionable «Войти» control. An enabled retry that can only re-fail violates the recovery/affordance expectation.
  - → Detect 401 specifically and surface a «Войти» action routing to login in both the submit error and the list ErrorState, instead of a generic message or a doomed retry.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-apps.jsx`

### forms-engine (18 тем)

- 🔴 **Drag-and-drop canvas blocks are not keyboard operable** _(M)_
  - In FormDesigner, each canvas block is a clickable/draggable <div> with no role, tabIndex, or keydown handler, and reordering is exclusively drag-driven with no keyboard/touch fallback and no drop-target indicator. The core authoring interaction (select, reorder) is entirely unreachable for keyboard-only and many motor-impaired users — a WCAG 2.1.1 failure on the screen's primary surface. Native HTML5 drag-and-drop has no keyboard equivalent.
  - → Make canvas nodes real controls (render the node header as a <button>, or role="button" tabIndex={0} aria-pressed with Enter/Space handling) and add explicit per-node move-up/move-down controls (reusing reorderNode) as the accessible reorder path. Add a visible insertion/drop indicator during dragover so placement is predictable for mouse users too.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`
- 🟠 **Raw HTML inputs/selects/checkboxes/buttons bypass kit primitives (<Field>/<Select>/<Button>)** _(L)_
  - Across the engine, controls are hand-rolled with literal className="chs-input"/"chs-btn" and inline styles instead of the contracted <Field>, <Select>, and <Button> primitives. field-renderer re-implements label binding, aria-required, aria-invalid and the required marker per-branch across ~10 control types (its single largest violation); FormBuilder and FormDesigner use raw checkboxes/inputs/textarea (often with broken or missing label association); FormDesigner, FormDocumentRenderer tabs, and FormDesigner palette/remove controls roll their own <button>. Hand-rolling drops focus-ring/aria/variant handling and lets branches drift.
  - → Route text/number/date/url/email/money inputs through <Field> and enums through <Select>; use <Button variant="ghost"> (with glyph prop) for palette/field/tab/remove/retry actions, layering drag attributes on top. Where the kit lacks a wrapper (checkbox, textarea, tabs), add the missing primitive to components.jsx (the contract flags these as real gaps) or at minimum bind the label programmatically and apply the kit focus-ring. This collapses duplicated label/error/required scaffolding and guarantees uniform a11y.
  - файлы: `src/forms/field-renderer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`
- 🟠 **Validation/field errors not programmatically tied to their inputs** _(M)_
  - Error text is rendered as detached sibling spans with no id, controls carry aria-invalid but no aria-describedby pointing at the message, and error spans lack role=alert/aria-live. Screen-reader users hear 'invalid' but never the reason, and errors injected after submit are not announced. This recurs in field-renderer (every control + PersonPicker), FormBuilder (processKey/stepKey/registry errors), FormDesigner (inspector), and FormDocumentRenderer (per-field). The multi-select checkbox group additionally has an orphaned htmlFor pointing at no element and no fieldset/role=group, so the group label and required/invalid state are lost entirely.
  - → Give each error span an id (e.g. `${id}-error`), set aria-describedby on the control when invalid, and add role=alert/aria-live=assertive to the span. Wrap multi-value checkbox groups in <fieldset><legend> (or role=group + aria-labelledby/aria-describedby/aria-required) and drop the dangling htmlFor. Adopting <Field>/<Select> fixes most of this for free since they wire error via aria-describedby.
  - файлы: `src/forms/field-renderer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`
- 🟠 **Hand-rolled Loading/Error/Empty states bypass kit primitives and offer no retry** _(L)_
  - Pending/success/error/empty states are bespoke <div>/<span> blocks instead of the contracted <LoadingState role=status aria-busy>, <ErrorState onRetry>, and <EmptyState>. The recurring harm is terminal, unrecoverable errors: PersonPicker's /api/org failure permanently blocks employee selection; FormViewer/FormDesigner save and load errors have no Повторить; FormBuilder mixes conventions (catalog load uses bare spans + hand-rolled retry button while apps/defs correctly use kit states). Loading states are often silent blank boxes (aria-busy with no role=status, spinner, or label) that read as broken, and missing descriptors are conflated with perpetual loading (Floor2Viewer) instead of a distinct Empty state.
  - → Standardize on the kit state primitives everywhere: <LoadingState>/<Skeleton> for pending (with visible affordance + role=status), <ErrorState onRetry={loadX}> for failures (lift each fetch into a callable so retry re-runs it), and <EmptyState> for genuinely-absent data — never conflated with loading. Scope load errors to their region rather than blowing away an in-progress canvas (FormDesigner registry-defs).
  - файлы: `src/forms/field-renderer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`, `src/forms/FormViewer.jsx`, `src/forms/Floor2Viewer.jsx`, `src/forms/Floor2Sandbox.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`
- 🟠 **Read-only <select>/checkbox use disabled — dropped from tab order and not submitted** _(M)_
  - In field-renderer, read-only mode applies disabled to selects and multi-select checkboxes while scalar inputs correctly use readOnly. disabled removes the control from keyboard tab order (a read-only field should remain focusable/readable) and a disabled select/checkbox is not serialized on native submit, so the value silently drops out of the form. Behaviour is now inconsistent across field types for the same mode.
  - → Make read-only uniform and keyboard-reachable across select, multi-select, checkbox and scalar inputs: render the resolved value as a read-only text readout in read-only mode, or keep the control focusable with aria-disabled + an onChange guard so the value still round-trips and stays in tab order. Never use disabled to express read-only.
  - файлы: `src/forms/field-renderer.jsx`
- 🟠 **Dev-jargon, raw field keys, spec refs and internal node types leak into user-facing copy** _(M)_
  - Internal vocabulary surfaces directly to end users across the engine, violating the no-dev-jargon principle and breaking error↔field mapping. Examples: FormViewer validation lists raw schema keys (vendor_inn, line_items) instead of human labels; Floor2Sandbox shows «§9.10» and «явный флаг»; Floor2Viewer/FormDocumentRenderer expose 'Floor-2', 'descriptor', 'узел', raw node.type and bindingKey (also in accessible names heard by screen readers); FormViewer renders verbatim 'HTTP 404/401' and raw err.message; FormBuilder/FormDesigner surface raw field.key and widget tokens as primary labels/placeholders. Users get unrecoverable, untranslated, non-actionable messages.
  - → Resolve every machine key/token to its human Russian label from the form schema before display; lead with field.title and demote the key to a mono subtitle/tooltip. Map HTTP status codes to plain copy with next steps (401→«Сессия истекла, войдите снова», 404→«Форма больше не доступна», network→«Не удалось связаться с сервером»). Drop spec refs, 'Floor-2'/'узел'/node.type and validator strings from the UI (log them to console/telemetry), and use the human form title for iframe title/aria-label instead of hard-coded ternaries.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormViewer.jsx`, `src/forms/Floor2Sandbox.jsx`, `src/forms/Floor2Viewer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`
- 🟠 **Hand-rolled tablist has incomplete keyboard nav and broken ARIA wiring** _(M)_
  - FormDocumentRenderer's TabsNode is bespoke buttons carrying role=tab/tablist/tabpanel but without Arrow/Home/End handling, roving tabindex, aria-controls/id linkage, or aria-labelledby on the panel. The half-built ARIA is actively misleading: it announces 'tab' semantics it does not fulfill, keyboard users get Tab-through-each-button instead of arrow-driven navigation, and screen readers cannot tie a panel to its tab. There is no <Tabs> primitive in the kit.
  - → Extract a shared <Tabs> primitive into components.jsx implementing the full APG pattern (roving tabindex, ArrowLeft/Right/Home/End, aria-controls→panel id, aria-labelledby on the panel) and consume it here, so every consumer gets correct semantics. At minimum add the id/aria-controls/aria-labelledby linkage and arrow-key navigation.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`
- 🟡 **Action outcomes shown inline instead of Toast; not assertive, focus-managed, or auto-cleared** _(M)_
  - Save/submit success and error outcomes render as static inline spans/paragraphs (role=status polite at best) rather than the contracted Toast surface. On long/scrolled forms these render off-screen so the user may submit and see no confirmation; failures use polite live regions that can be missed; FormDesigner's 'Сохранено.' never clears and goes stale after a later edit. No toast wiring (useToasts/ToastViewport) exists in these trees.
  - → Route outcomes through useToasts(): success → status/polite toast, failure → alert/assertive toast (announced and visible regardless of scroll). Wire ToastViewport at these trees. If an inline confirmation is kept, give it role=status/alert, move focus/scroll it into view on change, and clear it when the document changes after save.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`, `src/forms/FormViewer.jsx`
- 🟡 **Validation errors not linked back to the offending field/block** _(M)_
  - Multiple screens print validation errors as a flat list detached from the controls they refer to. FormDesigner computes brokenKeys but leaves it unused on the canvas, so authors must hunt for the failing block (and role=alert on a list that re-renders every keystroke is re-announced constantly); FormViewer lists errors in a panel below the sandboxed iframe with no way to focus/scroll to the bad field inside; FormDocumentRenderer scatters inline broken-binding boxes with no aggregate summary or resolution path.
  - → Mark the specific failing node/field inline (use brokenKeys for a danger border + per-node message) and make each summary item focus/scroll to (or postMessage the sandbox to focus) its control. Use role=status/polite for the live aggregate, reserving assertive announcements for on-submit so it isn't re-fired on every edit, and offer an optional broken-binding summary region linking to offending fields.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`, `src/forms/FormViewer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`
- 🟡 **Hardcoded colors, hex fallbacks, spacing, weights and fonts instead of semantic tokens** _(M)_
  - Token-contract violations recur: Floor2Viewer uses a non-existent --color-error with an always-applied #c00 fallback plus padding:'8px'/fontSize:'0.875rem' off the scale; Floor2Sandbox uses --chs-color-warning as a 1px dashed border (wrong status-token variant, no soft surface) and hardcodes #222/system-ui/14px inside the opaque srcdoc — which in dark theme renders dark-on-dark or unreadable; FormDesigner hardcodes save-color and a '×' glyph; FormDocumentRenderer uses raw fontWeight:600; and inline style objects scatter across field-renderer, Floor2Sandbox and others producing drifting paddings. Hardcoded #c00/#222 also bypass the WCAG-AA-verified theme palette.
  - → Consume only semantic --chs-* tokens: --chs-color-danger (no hex fallback), warning -soft/-border variants for passive notices, --chs-space-*, --chs-text-*, --chs-weight-semibold. For the opaque-origin iframe, read computed --chs-color-text/-bg/--chs-font-sans from the parent and inject them as inline CSS variables with an explicit background so dark-theme contrast stays AA. Move recurring layout into tokenized utility classes/primitives; keep only truly dynamic values (e.g. height) inline.
  - файлы: `src/forms/Floor2Viewer.jsx`, `src/forms/Floor2Sandbox.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`, `src/forms/field-renderer.jsx`
- 🟡 **Glyph/Unicode characters used as icons instead of Lucide currentColor icons** _(S)_
  - Several controls use literal text glyphs as icons, which the contract forbids (it mandates linear Lucide-style currentColor icons). FormBuilder reorder buttons use '↑'/'↓' (rendering inconsistently across platforms and clashing with KitIcon used elsewhere in the same file) and FormDesigner's remove control uses '×'.
  - → Replace text glyphs with <KitIcon name="arrow-up"/"arrow-down" /> (or chevrons) and a Lucide X, passed via the Button glyph prop, preserving existing aria-labels.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`
- 🟡 **Free-text key fields can silently bind to non-existent runtime values** _(M)_
  - In FormBuilder the step key is free text the user must type to exactly match the BPMN userTask step identifier, but validation only checks non-empty.
