A typo silently binds the form to a non-existent step with no feedback, producing a dead binding — the highest functional-correctness UX risk on the screen.
  - → Once a process is chosen, fetch its userTask step keys and offer them as a picker/datalist (like the existing process quick-pick), or validate the typed step against known steps and warn when it matches none. Free text should be a fallback, not the only path.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`
- ⚪ **State conveyed by opacity/color alone (use-of-color failures)** _(S)_
  - Several states are signalled only by dimming, a WCAG 1.4.1 failure that is invisible to screen-reader users, easy to miss for low-vision users, and can drop dimmed secondary text below AA contrast. FormDesigner field chips use opacity:0.5 for 'already used' with no text/icon/aria cue; FormBuilder excluded rows use opacity:0.45 plus a disabled input as the sole differentiator.
  - → Pair dimming with a non-color cue: an 'добавлено'/'выключено' label or checkmark icon, aria-disabled or a visually-hidden note, and verify the dimmed secondary text still meets AA (4.5:1) in light theme.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`, `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`
- ⚪ **Read-only/structural readouts and labels lack proper semantics and value** _(M)_
  - Read-only and structural surfaces are semantically weak and sometimes value-less. field-renderer renders relation/collection/date-range/file/rollup fields as italic prose inside a .chs-input box that looks editable but is a silent no-op (affordance violation), shows no actual value for read-only rollups, and misuses aria-readonly on a non-form <div>. FormDocumentRenderer's ReadoutNode and TableNode use a bare <label> with no htmlFor/association to the value or table.
  - → For read-only/rollup cases render the resolved formatted value in a neutral container (not .chs-input, not aria-readonly). For not-yet-authorable structural types make the message actionable (link/Button to where it is edited, or a «скоро» pattern). Associate readout/table labels with their value/table via aria-labelledby (or a definition-list/role=group pattern) rather than a free-floating <label>.
  - файлы: `src/forms/field-renderer.jsx`, `src/forms/FormDocumentRenderer.jsx`
- ⚪ **In-iframe submit not locked during pending request (double-submit risk)** _(S)_
  - FormViewer sets pending state and shows 'Отправка…' outside the iframe, but the actual submit button lives inside the sandboxed iframe and is never told about the pending state, so a user can press it again and trigger a second POST.
  - → postMessage a 'submitting' signal into the sandbox so the runtime disables/aria-busy's its submit button during the request and re-enables on completion, matching the kit's loading/aria-busy button convention.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormViewer.jsx`
- ⚪ **Required '*' marker has no text equivalent or legend** _(S)_
  - In field-renderer the required marker is a danger-colored '*' wrapped in aria-hidden (AT is covered via aria-required), but sighted users who can't distinguish the danger color see only an asterisk with no legend, and 'обязательное поле' text appears nowhere. The marker span is also duplicated inline across six branches.
  - → Keep aria-required, but pair the visual '*' with a form-level legend ('Поля со звёздочкой обязательны') or a non-color cue, and centralize the marker inside the shared <Field>/<Select> rather than re-emitting the styled span per branch.
  - файлы: `src/forms/field-renderer.jsx`
- ⚪ **Live preview buried in unstyled collapsed <details>** _(S)_
  - FormDesigner's live preview — the file's core value proposition ('identical to runtime') — is hidden in a default-collapsed native <details>/<summary> with no kit styling, no expand-affordance icon, and no indication it updates live, visually inconsistent with the kit-styled UI.
  - → Show the preview by default (or as a side-by-side pane); if disclosure is kept, style the summary with a chevron affordance and a 'reflects runtime' hint, and match kit typography/spacing.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormDesigner.jsx`
- ⚪ **Retry handlers duplicate load logic instead of reusing a callback** _(S)_
  - In FormBuilder the onRetry handlers for apps/defs re-implement the full fetch/parse/error pipeline inline, duplicating the effect bodies. Divergence would silently produce a retry that behaves differently from the initial load (different error mapping or reset).
  - → Extract loadApplications() and loadDefs(selectedAppId) as useCallback functions (mirroring loadCatalog) and call them from both the effect and onRetry, so the retry path is byte-identical to the initial load.
  - файлы: `src/forms/FormBuilder.jsx`

### observability-and-admin (19 тем)

- 🟠 **No success feedback after mutating actions (toast convention unused)** _(M)_
  - Across the area, successful save/publish/export/mark-read/mark-all/preference-save/refresh actions are silent. The design system ships useToasts()/<Toast>/<ToastViewport> for transient outcomes and the principles require an enabled action to do something observable, but these screens never wire it. Users (and screen readers) cannot tell whether a publish, bulk mark-all, preference toggle, or refresh actually succeeded — most acute on publish, a state transition with authz consequences.
  - → Wire useToasts()+<ToastViewport> once per screen tree and emit a polite success toast on every mutating outcome (saved/published/exported/marked/saved-settings) and on completed manual refresh. Reserve inline role=alert for errors. For non-destructive bulk actions (mark-all) optionally pair the toast with an Отменить.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-notifications.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-ops-overview.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-analytics.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-spend.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-org.jsx`
- 🟠 **Dev-jargon, raw IDs/UUIDs and machine code-names leak into the UI** _(M)_
  - Multiple screens surface internal taxonomy that the design-system principles explicitly forbid: raw action tokens (grant.role, agent.invoke) and target slugs in audit rows; raw event_kind keys (task.assigned, sla.breach) in notifications and settings; «explain-PDP», mcp://ledger.invoices and raw PDP step codes plus slug-as-name in the org executor card; raw process_key in analytics titles/options; and a 36-char page.id UUID as a report's primary title fallback. These are opaque and unprofessional for non-technical B2B admins.
  - → Render human labels as primary text everywhere (reuse existing maps like EVENT_KINDS; add server-side display-name fields for targets/processes/actors). Demote raw ids/slugs to a muted secondary line, Tooltip, or copy-on-click MonoId — never as the name or hero label. Replace UUID/empty fallbacks with «Без названия» / «Исполнитель не найден» and relabel engine vocabulary («explain-PDP» → «Проверка доступа»).
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-audit.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-notifications.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-org.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-analytics.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`
- 🟠 **Bespoke interactive elements lack proper ARIA roles and keyboard operability** _(L)_
  - Whole-surface and composite controls are reinvented without correct semantics or keyboard support. Tabs/sub-tabs and filter strips use plain buttons with aria-selected (invalid on a generic button) and no tablist/tab/tabpanel wiring or arrow-key nav; the org tree has no tree/treeitem roles, nests interactive controls (toggle, delete span) inside a native <button> (invalid HTML), and its expand toggle is mouse-only; the overview tiles are clickable <section>s with no role/tabIndex/key handler while nesting a focusable Button; the audit filter form has no group label. Keyboard and screen-reader users cannot operate or perceive these.
  - → Apply the correct WAI-ARIA pattern per control: tablist/tab/tabpanel + roving tabindex + arrow keys for tab strips; tree/treeitem/group + aria-level/aria-expanded/aria-selected + arrow keys for the org tree, with toggle/delete as sibling real <button>s outside the row button; make tile activation a single real link/button (or drop the section onClick); add aria-label/aria-labelledby to filter forms. Extract shared <Tabs>/<Tree> kit primitives so screens converge.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-notifications.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-org.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-overview.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-audit.jsx`
- 🟠 **Action/partial errors mishandled: bare divs, shared load-error collapse, stale errors, silent fall-through** _(M)_
  - Localized, recoverable failures are surfaced badly. The audit load-more error is a bare <div> (no role=alert, no retry) that is also never cleared on a later success; notification row-action and toggle errors share setError with the initial load and collapse the whole list into a full-screen ErrorState, wiping context; the org explain-PDP and load-more results are not wrapped in role=status/alert; and the reports publish flow does not return on promote failure, falling through to optimistically mark the row 'published' while the server rejected it — a dishonest contradictory state the user never sees because the builder unmounts.
  - → Separate load-error (full ErrorState) from action-error (scoped role=alert/ErrorState or toast that keeps content rendered). Use the kit ErrorState with onRetry for load-more, and setError(null) at the start of each attempt. On the reports promote failure, keep the builder open and surface submitErr, or read the server's true resulting tier rather than the optimistic flag — never claim 'published' on a rejected promote. Wrap async result regions in role=status/alert so transitions are announced.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-audit.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-notifications.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-org.jsx`
- 🟠 **Publish is an irreversible state transition with no confirmation** _(S)_
  - «Опубликовать» moves a report draft→published in one click, after which the «Изменить» affordance disappears (edit hidden when tier==='published'), making publication effectively irreversible from the UI — yet there is no confirmation step. The design system provides <ConfirmDialog> precisely for consequential actions.
  - → Gate «Опубликовать» behind a <ConfirmDialog> («Опубликовать отчёт? После публикации его нельзя будет изменить здесь.»), or provide an unpublish/edit-published path so the action is reversible.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`
- 🟠 **⌘K quick-search advertised but wired to nothing (silent no-op affordance)** _(S)_
  - The overview header shows a search icon, «Быстрый поиск раздела — ⌘K» and a styled <kbd>, but this file wires no keydown listener or command-palette trigger. If no global handler exists, it is a promised feature that silently fails (forbidden by the contract); even if one exists, the affordance is keyboard-only, unreachable on touch, and undiscoverable.
  - → Confirm a global ⌘K palette exists; if not, remove the hint or mark it «скоро». If it exists, make the hint an actual clickable trigger (kit <Button variant=ghost> opening the palette) so mouse/touch users can invoke it too.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-overview.jsx`
- 🟡 **Manual «Обновить» refresh blanks healthy data, no busy state, no last-updated indicator** _(M)_
  - On ops-overview, process-analytics and spend, the «Обновить» button tears down already-loaded content (remount via key / setData(null)) back to LoadingState — momentarily blanking healthy cards/tables — while the button itself never enters a busy/disabled state, inviting repeated and overlapping fetches. There is no aggregate progress signal, no completion confirmation, and no «обновлено в HH:MM» staleness indicator, which matters for analytics dashboards.
  - → Refresh in place (keep prior data visible under an aria-busy overlay) instead of collapsing to LoadingState; give the kit <Button> its loading/disabled state while fetches are in flight to block double-fires; and show a «Обновлено в HH:MM» timestamp (and/or completion toast) near the header.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-ops-overview.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-analytics.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-spend.jsx`
- 🟡 **Async state transitions not announced to assistive tech (missing aria-live/status)** _(M)_
  - Cards and panels swap LoadingState/ErrorState/EmptyState/data via conditional rendering inside static regions with no aria-live wrapper, so screen-reader users are not notified when content resolves (notably the data-success transition, which carries no announcement at all). Filter results, audit load-more outcomes, and the org explain verdict are likewise not announced. WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages.
  - → Wrap each state-swap container in an aria-live="polite" region (or set aria-busy while loading), ensure kit LoadingState/ErrorState carry their documented role=status/role=alert and sit inside that live region, and announce filter result counts via a polite role=status («Найдено N событий»).
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-ops-overview.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-audit.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-org.jsx`
- 🟡 **Money/numeric formatting: fixed 4 decimals, bare currency code, no grouping, non-mono font** _(M)_
  - Spend and ops-overview format money as n.toFixed(4) with a hand-built currency prefix ('RUB 0.0123', '$1234.5678'), no thousands grouping (inconsistent with token counts that use toLocaleString), misleading '$0.0000' for empty windows, and the values are rendered in the default sans stack despite the contract reserving --chs-font-mono for money/numeric columns. The product uses ₽, so a bare code prefix and 4-decimal precision scan poorly.
  - → Use Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', { style:'currency', currency }) (or the platform money helper) with locale grouping and a scale-aware decimal count (2 for large totals, more only for micro-costs), render '—' for empty windows, and apply --chs-font-mono / the kit <Mono> primitive to money and token/step counts.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-spend.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-ops-overview.jsx`
- 🟡 **Ad-hoc inline-styled surfaces/tables instead of kit primitives, with hardcoded px literals** _(L)_
  - Across the area, card chrome, tiles, and data tables are assembled from per-file inline-style objects rather than shared surfaces, and several files breach the token-only contract with raw px literals (monoStyle '1px 5px', maxWidth 940/200, bottleneck pill '1px 6px', grouped-table '4px 8px', report select minWidth 200, raw fontWeight:600, fixed 28px/320px, SVG fontSize 9). The absence of <Card>/<Panel>/<Table> primitives is sanctioned, but repeated ad-hoc scaffolds invite border/padding/casing drift across the growing observability surface and bypass theming.
  - → Replace raw px/weight literals with --chs-space / size / weight tokens; consume the kit <Mono>/<MonoId> and existing chip primitives (e.g. <DerivedChip> for the bottleneck marker) instead of hand-rolled styles; extract shared local Card/MiniRow/table-style modules per screen; and propose lightweight <Panel>/<DataTable> primitives to the design-system owners to stop cross-screen drift.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-spend.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-ops-overview.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-analytics.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-org.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-overview.jsx`
- 🟡 **Raw inputs/selects and non-kit buttons bypass the kit (inconsistent focus/disabled/type)** _(M)_
  - Several controls bypass the design system: bare <input type=checkbox> in the notifications settings matrix (no <Checkbox> wrapper, kit focus-ring or busy state), a raw native <select> for the reports app picker, and hand-rolled <button>s (notification row action, tab strips) mixed beside the kit <Button>. These diverge in focus-ring, disabled/aria-busy handling, and many omit explicit type="button" (default submit inside a form would trigger an unintended reload).
  - → Consume kit primitives where they exist (<Select label=...> for the app picker; <Button variant=ghost size=sm> for the row action) and add type="button" to every remaining hand-rolled button; for the checkbox matrix, introduce/consume a kit <Checkbox> (or at minimum inherit kit focus-ring/disabled tokens) and add aria-busy/spinner during in-flight saves.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-notifications.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`
- 🟡 **Counter/stat loading and empty-vs-error ambiguity on the home overview** _(M)_
  - On the overview tiles, fetchCount collapses every failure to null and renders an ambiguous «—» (reads as unavailable but may be misread as zero) with no retry path and no aria label, so a screen reader announces a bare em dash. The loading spinner sits in the number slot with no reserved width (layout shift) and LoadingState label="" strips its accessible status text; two tiles also render an empty stats row, misaligning CTA baselines.
  - → Give «—» an accessible label/title («данные недоступны») and a lightweight retry (header refresh or refetch on focus/visibility); use a number-shaped <Skeleton> with a real visually-hidden loading label instead of an unsized spinner; and skip rendering the stats row when empty so all tiles share one CTA baseline.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-overview.jsx`
- 🟡 **Timestamp presentation: no date grouping and unverified epoch/invalid guards** _(S)_
  - The audit log spans days/weeks but renders a single time/timestamp column per row with no day grouping/separators, hurting scannability and day-attribution; the formatter must also be verified to return a placeholder rather than 1970-01-01 / Invalid Date for null ts (principles forbid epoch leaks).
  - → Group events under day headers («Сегодня», «Вчера», then dd.MM.yyyy) with time-only within a group in --chs-font-mono, and confirm fmtTs returns «—» for null/invalid timestamps instead of an epoch date.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-audit.jsx`
- ⚪ **Generic delete confirmation and lost focus/selection after CRUD in the org tree** _(M)_
  - The org delete ConfirmDialog uses a generic «Удалить?» for departments/positions/employees/roles alike and never warns about cascade dependents (FK_IN_USE is only learned via an error toast after confirming). After a successful create the modal closes with only a transient toast: focus is not returned to the trigger and the new node is not selected/scrolled-to, so keyboard users lose their place and there is no persistent visual confirmation.
  - → Make the confirm title entity-specific («Удалить подразделение?») and surface a pre-check/warning line about possible dependents; on create, return focus to the invoking control and auto-select/scroll to the newly created node so the action has a persistent visible result.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-org.jsx`
- ⚪ **401 / error states offer no actionable recovery path (dead-end retry)** _(S)_
  - On spend and process-analytics, a 401 renders ErrorState with copy «Войдите в систему» but only a «Повторить» action that re-fires the same unauthenticated fetch and 401s again — a dead-end. Process-analytics also hides the process selector during error, trapping the user on a failing scope with no way back to «Все процессы».
  - → Give the 401 branch an actionable CTA/link into the login flow (or rely on the shell's global auth redirect) instead of only «Повторить», and keep the process selector visible/enabled during error states (driven by last-known keys) so users can switch scope to recover.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-spend.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-analytics.jsx`
- ⚪ **Missing semantic headings and table header scope for document/grid structure** _(S)_
  - Overview tiles use styled <span> titles with no <h1> page title and no <h2> tile headings, removing heading-based navigation. Several ad-hoc tables (notifications preference matrix, reports grouped/metric tables) omit scope="col"/scope="row" on th cells, weakening the programmatic grid relationship for screen readers. WCAG 1.3.1.
  - → Add a (visually subtle) <h1> per screen and promote tile titles to <h2> (or aria-labelledby the section to its heading), and add scope="col" to column headers and scope="row" row-header th to data tables.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-overview.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-notifications.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`
- ⚪ **Charts/bars encode data visually with weak accessible fallback and silent truncation** _(S)_
  - The reports bar chart has only a generic SVG aria-label, exposes bar values/labels as <text> SRs don't reliably read, hard-truncates group labels to 12 chars with no title/tooltip, and uses an off-scale fontSize=9; the analytics DurationBar is aria-hidden with its relative-to-slowest meaning never stated in the UI. Both are acceptable only because adjacent text/tables carry the values.
  - → Where an accessible table already carries the data, mark the chart aria-hidden; otherwise add <title> per bar/label with the full untruncated key+value, replace off-scale font sizes with type tokens, and add a one-line caption explaining the relative encoding (e.g. «Полоса — доля от самого долгого шага»).
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-process-analytics.jsx`
- ⚪ **Free-text actor filter expecting an exact slug/id with no typeahead or unmatched hint** _(M)_
  - The audit actor filter is a free-text field whose placeholder «slug или id актора» is itself jargon, requires the operator to know an exact identifier, has no autocomplete/validation, and on a typo produces the generic «Ничего не найдено» empty state with no hint that the actor string was unmatched.
  - → Prefer a typeahead/Select populated with known actors (humans/agents/services); if free text must remain, soften the placeholder to «Имя или логин» and, on a zero-result filtered query, hint that the typed actor may not exist rather than only «Измените или сбросьте фильтры».
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-audit.jsx`
- ⚪ **Microcopy clarity: cryptic abbreviations, asymmetric export labels, weak Reports signal, full-takeover builder** _(M)_
  - Smaller content-clarity issues: the spend table abbreviates token columns to «Вх / Исх» with no legend; reports has asymmetric export labels («Выгрузить XLSX» vs bare «CSV») in an undifferentiated three-ghost-button cluster and hides the whole report list during editing (losing reference context); the ops-overview Reports card presents an app count as a hero metric that is a weak proxy for 'reports' and may mislead.
  - → Expand «Вх/Исх» to «Вход. токены / Исход. токены» (or add th tooltips); make export labels parallel or use a single «Выгрузить ▾» split button and render the report builder as a <Drawer> so the list stays visible; and reframe the Reports card as an entry point (lead with recent-apps + CTA, drop the count hero) or surface a genuine report metric.
  - файлы: `src/screens/screen-spend.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-reports.jsx`, `src/screens/screen-ops-overview.jsx`

### process-canvas (15 тем)

- 🟠 **Canvas keyboard navigation disabled and diagram opaque to assistive tech** _(M)_
  - The core BPMN editing surface is keyboard-inaccessible and unannounced. The modeler is instantiated with keyboard.bindTo=null ('disabled for now'), so the canvas cannot be panned/zoomed/undone or have selections moved via keyboard (WCAG 2.1.1 Level A). The mount div also has no role, no aria-label, and no text alternative for the SVG diagram, so a screen-reader user reaching the primary authoring surface hears nothing meaningful (WCAG 4.1.2 / 1.1.1). For a no-code B2B authoring canvas this is a hard accessibility gap on the central interaction.
  - → Bind bpmn-js keyboard to the container (keyboard: { bindTo: containerRef.current }) so canvas actions are keyboard-operable, and remove the 'for now' placeholder as a deliberate decision. Add role="application" (or role="img") plus a named aria-label like 'Редактор диаграммы процесса' (accept the process name via prop), and render a visually-hidden text summary of steps/executors as the SVG's text alternative.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/bpmn-modeler-wrapper.jsx`
- 🟠 **Form labels not programmatically associated with their controls** _(M)_
  - Across the properties panels, native <input>/<select> controls pair with a visual <label> by proximity only — no htmlFor/id binding, aria-label, or aria-labelledby. ux-patterns review_code flagged this repeatedly (8 in bpmn-properties-panel, 3 in gateway-condition-panel, 1 in timer-deadline-panel, plus the agent-task autonomy select). Screen readers announce unlabeled comboboxes/textboxes and clicking the label does not focus the control (WCAG 1.3.1 / 4.1.2). This is the direct downstream consequence of bypassing the kit primitives.
  - → Bind every control to its label: give each a stable id and set htmlFor on the matching label (or aria-labelledby). The cleanest fix is to route controls through the kit <Field>/<Select>, which handle label↔control binding automatically; where the bpmn-js chrome must be preserved, add the id/htmlFor wiring manually.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/agent-task-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/timer-deadline-panel.jsx`
- 🟠 **Raw <input>/<select>/<button> instead of design-system kit primitives** _(L)_
  - Every panel hand-rolls native controls with bpmn-js bio-properties-panel-* classes instead of the kit <Field>/<Select>/<Button>, which the contract explicitly names as a consumption violation. This appears in the agent-task autonomy select (next to a sibling that correctly uses kit <Select>), the entire General/Outcome/SequenceFlow editors and bespoke add/remove buttons, both gateway inputs, and all four timer controls. The cost is inconsistent focus-ring, no aria-invalid/hint path, and broken label binding. Several panels mount inside the third-party bpmn-js chrome, so full adoption may be context-bound, but the same control type is correctly kit-bound elsewhere in these very files (e.g. the 'Агент' select, MessageCorrelation/UserTaskForm fields).
  - → Migrate controls to kit <Field> (text), <Select> (dropdowns) and <Button variant=…> (actions), removing inline styles so focus-ring, aria-invalid and theming come from the kit. Where the bpmn-js DOM contract genuinely forbids it, document the exception per-site and at minimum wire the aria/label bindings the kit would have provided.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/agent-task-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/timer-deadline-panel.jsx`
- 🟠 **Destructive 'Удалить исход' deletes immediately with no confirmation or undo** _(S)_
  - Clicking 'Удалить исход' filters the outcome from state and commits to the businessObject in one click, with no ConfirmDialog and no toast. A configured branch — with its routing target, requiresComment and confirm flags — can be wiped by a single mis-click, and the author gets zero feedback that it happened or how to revert. The contract requires <ConfirmDialog> for silent destructive actions plus a Toast for transient outcomes.
  - → Gate removal behind <ConfirmDialog> for outcomes that carry non-default config, or route the change through bpmn-js modeling so it joins the undo/redo stack, and surface a kit Toast ('Исход удалён') on completion.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`
- 🟠 **Group accordion headers are non-semantic divs unreachable by keyboard** _(S)_
  - Every collapsible properties-panel section header is a <div role="button" aria-expanded onClick> with no tabIndex and no onKeyDown, flagged by both MCP tools (review_code + a 2.1.1 critical). Keyboard and screen-reader users cannot expand or collapse any section (Общее, Назначение, Исходы шага, etc.), making large parts of the panel unoperable.
  - → Replace the header div with a real <button type="button"> (full-width, transparent, reusing the existing class) keeping aria-expanded, which gives free Enter/Space activation and focus-ring. If a div must remain for layout, add tabIndex={0} and an onKeyDown handler for Enter/Space.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`
- 🟠 **Validation errors silently suppressed or surfaced only at publish time** _(M)_
  - Required/invalid configurations produce no inline feedback and only fail at publish. The timer's 'Укажите срок.' error is computed but never rendered for the most common mistake (a blank deadline), because the render guard requires a non-empty value. Selecting 'Конкретная роль…' with no role chosen, or 'Конкретная роль' escalation, commits an invalid empty config silently. The agent-task reads/writes free-text key fields accept typos with no validation against the field catalog, deferring the failure to runtime. The gateway allows a no-default state with no actionable call-to-action.
  - → Add touched/blur-tracked inline validation that surfaces required-field gaps before publish: show the empty-deadline error once touched, validate the dependent role picker ('Выберите роль'), and validate against the record field catalog for reads/writes keys. Associate each message via aria-invalid/aria-describedby, and mark incomplete entries so authors see gaps in-panel rather than at publish.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/timer-deadline-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/agent-task-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`
- 🟡 **Edits auto-commit silently with no save/failure feedback, and write errors are swallowed** _(M)_
  - Every panel writes straight to the businessObject and fires element.changed with no confirmation of persistence — no toast, no 'сохранено'/dirty indicator. Off-canvas config (outcomes, message correlation, BYO endpoint, role, agent reads/writes, gateway cascades) gives the author no signal the change stuck. Worse, write failures are swallowed (fireChanged try/caught, gateway catch (_) {}), so a failed save is indistinguishable from success. Cascading gateway actions (clearing a flow's condition on default-set, rewriting N branch conditions on variable rename) deserve explicit signal.
  - → Keep implicit-save but surface outcomes: wrap writes in try/catch and raise a kit error <Toast role=alert> on failure (never swallow), add a subtle panel-level 'изменения применяются к процессу' / 'сохранено в схеме' affordance, and emit a transient confirmation for cascading mutations (e.g. «Условия N веток обновлены»).
  - файлы: `src/canvas/agent-task-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`
- 🟡 **Load-failure error states lack a retry affordance** _(S)_
  - Non-401 load failures render dead-end apologies that tell the user to 'Повторите позже' but offer no control to do so, and the fetchedRef guard blocks any re-fetch. This affects the agent-task panel's agents-load error and the roles-load error in the properties panel. The contract's error convention is role=alert PLUS a «Повторить» action (ErrorState onRetry).
  - → Expose a retry path from the data hooks (reset the fetched guard + loading) and render failures via kit <ErrorState onRetry={retry}> (or at least a ghost <Button>Повторить</Button>), dropping the 'Повторите позже' wording once a real retry exists.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/agent-task-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`
- 🟡 **Loading / empty / error sub-states use bare <p> instead of kit state primitives** _(M)_
  - Roles-loading and no-roles/empty states render plain paragraphs with no role=status/aria-busy, diverging from the contract's explicit-state convention (Loading=role=status aria-busy, Empty=role=status, EmptyState + CTA). This appears as bare 'Загрузка ролей…' / 'Роли недоступны' text in the properties and timer panels, the hand-rolled no-selection EmptyState, and the gateway's no-outgoing-flows paragraph. None are announced to assistive tech and they are visually inconsistent with the rest of the app. Separately, the modeler wrapper shows no loading affordance during async importXML, leaving a blank canvas with no aria-busy while bpmn-js parses/lays out.
  - → Use the kit <LoadingState> (role=status aria-busy) and <EmptyState> (role=status + CTA) for these sub-states, or at minimum add role=status/aria-busy to the existing nodes. In the modeler wrapper, set aria-busy on the container until importXML settles and either overlay a <LoadingState>/<Skeleton> or expose a loading flag so the parent keeps its LoadingState mounted until onReady fires.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/timer-deadline-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/bpmn-modeler-wrapper.jsx`
- 🟡 **Dev-jargon, internal identifiers and element IDs leak into user-facing UI** _(M)_
  - The panels surface implementation identifiers the contract forbids for the no-code audience: raw BPMN element ids appended to gateway branch labels (Flow_1a2b3c) and embedded in the default-new diagram; moddle/attribute names and raw $type strings in properties labels ('Имя исхода (choros:outcomeName)', 'bpmn:ServiceTask', 'bpmn:UserTask'); internal table/column names in message-correlation labels and placeholders ('effect_resource id', 'messaging_channel uuid'); and inherently technical ISO-8601 / PT24H deadline formats. These are confusing implementation concepts, not domain terms.
  - → Strip implementation identifiers from primary labels/placeholders, replacing them with plain-language domain copy and friendly fallbacks (e.g. «Ветка 1» or the target-node name instead of Flow ids; 'Имя исхода (ветки)'; 'Канал отправки' with a human placeholder). Move any technical id needed for debugging into a tooltip/MonoId. For the default-new canvas, start minimal (Start→End + EmptyState hint) and gate the rich invoice example behind an explicit 'вставить пример'. Consider structured duration/date inputs for timers, keeping raw ISO for advanced cases.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/bpmn-modeler-wrapper.jsx`, `src/canvas/timer-deadline-panel.jsx`
- 🟡 **Hardcoded hex/weight fallbacks and inline semantic styling bypass the token contract** _(S)_
  - Several styles embed raw hex fallbacks inside var() defaults (#c00 on danger across multiple panels, #2563eb on a possibly-undefined --chs-color-brand, #b45309 on warning) and raw values (fontWeight: 600). The contract treats hardcoded color/size and consuming undefined tokens as violations: a missing token should fall back to another defined semantic token, not a hex literal, and the brand reference may render off-palette blue defeating dark-theme WCAG-AA. Inline color styling for semantic error/warning text also scatters one-off styles and bypasses the kit's <ErrorState>/<Toast> presentation.
  - → Drop all hex/weight literals and consume canonical semantic tokens directly (var(--chs-color-danger), var(--chs-color-accent) for accent actions, var(--chs-color-warning), var(--chs-weight-semibold)); verify --chs-color-brand and --chs-color-surface-alt exist, else switch to the documented tokens. Route semantic error/warning text through kit primitives so the danger styling and role come from the kit rather than inline attributes.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/agent-task-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/timer-deadline-panel.jsx`
- 🟡 **Validation/strip warnings and errors not announced as live regions or linked to fields** _(S)_
  - Inline warnings and errors are plain paragraphs with no role=alert/status/aria-live and no aria-invalid/aria-describedby linking them to their fields. The gateway's quote/backslash strip-warning is unannounced and the char is silently stripped on persist so displayed and stored values diverge; the timer's deadline error has no role and the input no aria-invalid/describedby for either error or hint (WCAG 3.3.1 / 4.1.3). Assistive tech gets no signal a field is in error or what the message says.
  - → Give warning/error containers role=status (polite) or role=alert as appropriate, set aria-invalid on the relevant input, and reference hint+error nodes via aria-describedby — or route through kit <Field hint error> which wires this. For the gateway, echo the sanitized value back so the field shows what is actually stored.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`, `src/canvas/timer-deadline-panel.jsx`
- 🟡 **Import failure leaves a deceptively normal blank canvas with no in-wrapper recovery** _(S)_
  - On importXML rejection the modeler wrapper logs and calls the optional onError, then still renders its empty container. There is no retry in the wrapper itself and onError is typeof-checked optional, so a future caller that forgets it silently reverts to a blank canvas indistinguishable from an empty diagram. The error is also a fixed Russian UI string built inside the canvas wrapper, mixing presentation into the data layer.
  - → Make the contract safer: warn in dev when importXML rejects with no onError handler provided, pass a structured error (code + raw message) so the presentation layer owns copy, and ensure the parent renders kit <ErrorState role=alert onRetry={remount}> with a real re-import retry.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/bpmn-modeler-wrapper.jsx`
- 🟡 **Default-branch radio group not reversible and not grouped for assistive tech** _(S)_
  - Each gateway branch row carries a standalone radio sharing name="gateway-default". Once any branch is marked default there is no affordance to return to the 'no default' state (radios are not individually deselectable), yet the model can legitimately start in a no-default state with no call-to-action to fix it. There is also no fieldset/legend grouping the radios, so the relationship to the «exactly one default» constraint is not conveyed to assistive tech.
  - → Wrap the per-row radios in a <fieldset><legend>Ветка по умолчанию</legend> and add an explicit «Нет ветки по умолчанию» option so the state is reversible; if a default is required, validate and surface an inline ErrorState/warning when none is selected rather than relying on prose.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/gateway-condition-panel.jsx`
- ⚪ **Checkboxes hand-rolled with inline styling and nesting-only label association** _(S)_
  - Checkboxes use raw <input type="checkbox"> wrapped in styled <label>s with inline fontSize/cursor across OutcomeRow flags and the message-correlation broadcast toggle. The kit has no <Checkbox> primitive (a noted absence), so this is partly unavoidable, but the inline styling is duplicated across components, the controls miss the kit focus-ring token, and the OutcomeRow boxes rely solely on adjacent text for their name.
  - → Flag a kit <Checkbox> primitive as follow-up; in the interim factor a single local Checkbox helper so styling/focus-ring/association are consistent, and ensure each input carries an explicit id+htmlFor or aria-label.
  - файлы: `src/canvas/bpmn-properties-panel.jsx`

### rights-and-access (16 тем)

- 🔴 **Destructive security actions fire with no confirmation** _(M)_
  - Irreversible, high-blast-radius rights operations commit on a single click with no confirmation step or undo. ra-intents fires «Уволить» (revokes ALL of an employee's grants/assignments atomically) and «Срочно отозвать» (kills a grant and halts a running agent) directly from submit. ra-criticality approves/rejects dual-control change requests on one click. ra-role-editor deletes grants and rejects LLM proposals with no confirm. ra-sod uses native window.confirm() (a contract violation) for deleting separation-of-duties rules, with no role-pair context. The kit ships <ConfirmDialog> precisely to gate these flows and it is unused.
  - → Gate every destructive/consequential rights action behind the kit <ConfirmDialog> (danger variant) with an explicit, named summary of consequences and who/what is affected (e.g. «Снять все права у П. Петров? Действие необратимо.», or the specific role pair for SoD). Replace all window.confirm() calls. For dual-control reject, embed the reason field in the dialog. Where confirmation is heavy, alternatively provide an undo Toast.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`
- 🟠 **Dev-jargon leaks into user-facing UI (UUIDs, JSON, raw codes, code-names, raw timestamps)** _(L)_
  - Across the section, implementation detail is rendered verbatim to non-technical rights administrators, violating the no-dev-jargon principle and the G5 honest-text gate. ra-sod requires typing role UUIDs and hand-authoring a JSON scope object, echoes raw subjectId, and shows truncated UUID 'ID' columns; ra-intents asks admins to hand-type a 36-char grant UUID, dumps full <pre>JSON.stringify(payload)</pre> on success, and surfaces raw server codes (SUBSTITUTION_WIDENS, HTTP 500, «статус ${state}», «Tier-${tier}»); ra-role-editor prints raw role UUIDs, a code expression hint, a hardcoded fake reviewer name 'М. Соколов', and 'scope-set из N узлов'; ra-data carries grant IDs (grt-…), raw mcp:// URIs, and ms-precision timestamps; ra-grant-trail renders mislabeled UTC ISO timestamps with milliseconds (and literal 'null'/'undefined'), JSON.stringify of non-node scopes, and 'hierarchy:nodeId'; screen-rights and ra-shell expose role-id code-names and the infra term 'control-plane'.
  - → Establish one mapping/formatting layer for the section: replace UUID free-text inputs with name-keyed Select/pickers; map server enums and error codes to plain-Russian copy (raw code only as secondary detail); render human labels for ops/resources/scopes via shared formatters; format timestamps locale-aware in the stated timezone without milliseconds and with a '—' placeholder for null; remove JSON dumps entirely; hide raw IDs (or expose only via a <MonoId>/copy affordance); and replace 'control-plane' with a human admin label.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-data.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/screen-rights.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-shell.jsx`
- 🟠 **Form labels not bound to controls; raw inputs/selects/textareas bypass <Field>/<Select>** _(M)_
  - Form controls across the section are hand-rolled <input>/<select>/<textarea> with labels placed adjacently but not programmatically associated (no htmlFor/id), so screen readers announce unnamed fields and label clicks don't focus controls. ra-sod has bare labels on the rule-type select, both role inputs, scope textarea, and a <span> 'label' on the Сотрудник input. ra-role-editor has a numeric range input named only by a decorative '≤ ₽' span, an LLM textarea named only by placeholder, and a free-text second-confirmer login field with no binding/validation prefilled with a dev slug. ra-intents hand-rolls the <Field> wrapper inconsistently — OrgPicker uses useId but the inline Тип/Пресет selects have no id/htmlFor. review_code flagged these as real missing-label errors. The kit <Field>/<Select> primitives are bypassed.
  - → Route all inputs through the kit <Field> and selects through a <Select> primitive (or centralize select rendering in one helper, and file the kit <Select> gap). Ensure every control has a bound label (htmlFor/id) or an aria-label, the textarea gets a real label not a placeholder, and the second-confirmer is a Select of eligible admins rather than free text with a dev-slug default.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`
- 🟠 **Missing or dishonest Loading/Empty/Error states; errors swallowed with no retry** _(L)_
  - The contract mandates every list/detail render explicit Loading/Empty/Error states, but they are missing, hand-rolled, or silently swallowed. ra-grant-trail never consumes its loading flag, swallows fetch errors and silently presents stale seed rows as a live audit log, uses a bare-div empty state with no role=status/CTA, and empties loadMore failures into .catch(()=>{}). ra-role-editor renders loading/empty/error as ad-hoc inline-styled divs with raw '{error}' (HTTP 500) and no onRetry. ra-intents shows error as a plain colored div with no role=alert and no Повторить, ignores its computed loading/empty flags for presets and org directory, and surfaces raw server codes. ra-sod renders delete errors as an unannounced raw inline string with no retry. ra-shell mounts sub-screens with no error boundary or loading fallback, so a throwing/slow screen goes blank.
  - → Use the kit state primitives everywhere: <LoadingState role=status aria-busy>/<Skeleton> while fetching, <ErrorState onRetry={refetch}> (role=alert) with human-mapped messages on failure, and <EmptyState role=status> with a recovery CTA. Never silently fall back to seed/stale data — show an honest 'demo data, journal unavailable' banner if a fallback is intentional. Add an error boundary at the shell switch point. Re-enable retry on loadMore failures via an assertive Toast.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-shell.jsx`
- 🟠 **Tab/segmented/toggle controls don't expose role or pressed/selected state to AT** _(L)_
  - Selectable controls convey state only via CSS class or with ARIA attributes invalid on their element. ra-shell and ra-grant-trail put aria-selected on plain <button>s inside a plain <div> with no role=tablist/tab, no roving tabindex, and no arrow-key navigation — invalid ARIA that AT ignores, so users can't tell which section/filter is active. ra-data's Segmented and AxisList toggles, ra-data CriticalityBadge axes, ra-role-editor op/scope/tag/preset multi-select toggles, and ra-grant-trail's «только критичные» toggle convey on/off only through CSS classes with no aria-pressed (or role=checkbox/aria-checked). The bespoke RightsSubTabs / chs-tabs / Segmented idioms duplicate each other since no shared <Tabs> primitive exists.
  - → For view switchers, implement the full tabs pattern (role=tablist/tab, aria-selected, roving tabindex, arrow keys, role=tabpanel + aria-labelledby) or model content filters as a radiogroup/toggle-button group with aria-pressed. Add aria-pressed (or aria-checked) to every multi-select/toggle control and include state in the accessible name. Extract a shared <Tabs>/<Segmented> primitive so RightsSubTabs, chs-tabs, and Segmented converge.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-shell.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-data.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`
- 🟠 **Enabled controls that are silent no-ops, or disabled controls with no explanation** _(M)_
  - The affordance rule (an enabled element must do something, else be disabled/«скоро») is violated repeatedly. ra-grant-trail's Экспорт button has no onClick yet renders fully enabled — a primary audit action that silently fails. ra-role-editor and screen-rights render a fake «Поиск роли» that looks like a search box but is a static <div> with no input/handler. Conversely, disabled controls give no reason: ra-criticality disables every row's buttons globally on one in-flight action (reads as frozen UI); ra-shell hard-disables real-looking nav items (with count badges) using only a title tooltip; ra-intents leaves submit buttons disabled with no indication of which required field is missing; screen-rights conveys a disabled «Запросить изменение» reason only via native title (lost to keyboard/AT).
  - → Make every enabled control do something or mark it disabled/aria-disabled with a visible «скоро» affordance and keyboard-reachable explanation (aria-describedby/Tooltip, not native title). Wire Экспорт or disable it; make the search fields real <Field> filters or remove them. Scope in-flight disabling to the acting row. Mark required fields (aria-required) and explain what blocks a disabled submit. Hide count badges on dead nav items.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/screen-rights.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-shell.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`
- 🟠 **Static lists/whitelists hide data; counts disagree with visible rows** _(M)_
  - Lists are driven by hardcoded structures instead of API data, silently dropping rows and producing counts that don't match what's shown. screen-rights renders only roles whose id is in a hardcoded ROLE_GROUPS dev-id whitelist — any role outside the three departments silently vanishes (unreachable, no «прочие» bucket), new tenant roles never appear until code is edited, and the header {roles.length} can disagree with visible rows. ra-grant-trail computes tab counts from allRows ignoring the critOnly toggle, so a tab can read '42' while the table shows 3 with no explanation. ra-data's TRAIL mock references resources/ops absent from the canonical catalog, so RES_BY_URI lookups fall back to raw URIs.
  - → Drive grouping and counts from the API payload (group by a data field, render an «Прочее» bucket for ungrouped roles so every counted role is reachable), and compute filter/tab counts against the same predicate as the visible rows (or show 'N из M'). Reconcile mock data (TRAIL resources/ops) against the canonical catalog so consumers can always resolve a human label.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/screen-rights.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-data.jsx`
- 🟠 **Dual-control reject captures no justification despite API support** _(S)_
  - In ra-criticality, decideChangeRequest(id, action, reason) accepts and forwards a reason to the dual-control API, but handleReject always passes null — the reason field is wired end-to-end yet unreachable from the UI. For an access-rights denial in a dual-control security flow this is both a UX gap (the initiator gets no explanation) and an audit gap.
  - → When rejecting, prompt for a reason (a <Field>/textarea inside the ConfirmDialog from the confirmation theme) and pass it through to decideChangeRequest, making it required or at least offered, so denials carry an explanation back to the initiator and into the audit log.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`
- 🟡 **App-content controls and primitives hand-rolled instead of consuming the kit** _(L)_
  - Screens re-roll components the kit already provides (or should), producing inconsistent focus rings, hover, disabled/loading semantics, and token divergence. ra-sod uses raw <button className=chs-btn> with literal '…'/'Сохранение…' instead of <Button loading>, plus a hand-built raised card. ra-role-editor uses bare <button> for op/scope/tag/preset/'+ грант'/proposal actions instead of <Button>. ra-criticality hand-rolls chip variants (chs-chip--grant/--assignment) outside the documented chip set, and ra-data hand-rolls SectionHead, Segmented, ScopeToken, ProvenanceTag, CriticalityBadge — with Segmented overlapping the shell's bespoke RightsSubTabs (two tab idioms in one section). The contract notes no <Tabs>/<Card>/<Badge>/<Select> primitives exist, so each screen inventing its own raises the flagged inconsistency risk.
  - → Swap raw <button> for kit <Button> (with loading/glyph props), route chips through the documented chip family or promote a typed chip into the kit, and consolidate the section's bespoke primitives (SectionHead/Segmented/tokens/badges) into one shared, documented set so sibling screens import rather than re-roll. Where a primitive is genuinely missing (Tabs, Select, Card, Badge, Checkbox), file the kit gap and converge on one canonical implementation.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-data.jsx`
- 🟡 **Ad-hoc div/span data tables lack semantic table roles** _(M)_
  - Tabular rights data is rendered as CSS-grid divs/spans with no table semantics, so screen-reader users get a flat stream of cells with no column-header association and no row/column navigation. ra-grant-trail's 8-column audit log, screen-rights's 3-column grant matrix (Ресурс/Операции/Охват), and ra-criticality's request rows are all hand-assembled without role=table/row/columnheader/cell. The contract notes there is no shared <Table> primitive, so each screen assembles one ad hoc without relationship semantics.
  - → Add ARIA grid/table semantics (role=table/row/columnheader/cell, or a real <table> with scope) so headers associate with cells, especially for the audit log and grant matrix. Longer-term, extract a shared <DataTable> primitive so the rights screens stop hand-rolling tables.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/screen-rights.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`
- 🟡 **Meaning carried by glyphs/icons/color with no text or AT equivalent** _(M)_
  - Meaning-bearing visuals lack a text/AT fallback, against the rule that glyphs must duplicate with text and meaning must not rest on color alone. ra-data renders criticality axes as single letters explained only by a native title tooltip (unreachable by keyboard/touch). ra-grant-trail uses one-vs-two KitIcon success checkmarks to signal single vs dual sign-off with no accessible name — losing a compliance signal. screen-rights shows holder executor type via an 8px ExecGlyph whose only title is the name, not the type. ra-data's and ra-sod's decorative glyph spans/divs aren't marked aria-hidden, and raw '≤'/'#' operator characters read out of context. ra-grant-trail's «критичные» colored dot and ra-sod's severity chips risk color-only cues (though several pair text, which is good).
  - → Give every meaning-bearing glyph/icon an accessible name (aria-label or visible/visually-hidden text) and convey the distinction (single vs dual sign-off, executor type, axis state) in text — not hover-only or icon-count-only. Mark purely decorative glyphs aria-hidden, pair operator characters with words ('не более' for ≤), and ensure all state has a non-color cue.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-data.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/screen-rights.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`
- 🟡 **Transient outcomes shown inline/lost instead of via Toast** _(M)_
  - Action outcomes use the wrong surface or get lost. ra-criticality wires <ToastViewport> in only one of four render branches, so the most consequential confirmation — clearing the last pending request, which re-renders into the empty branch — shows no toast. ra-sod emits no success Toast on create/delete of governance-critical rules (only absence of error). ra-intents and ra-role-editor render save/confirm outcomes as persistent inline banners/spans that can scroll out of view and aren't assertively announced for errors. The contract designates Toast (polite for success, assertive/role=alert for error) for transient outcomes via useToasts()+ToastViewport.
  - → Hoist <ToastViewport> to each screen's always-rendered root and emit outcomes through useToasts(): polite success toasts (create/delete/apply) and assertive role=alert error toasts, rather than branch-local viewports or persistent inline banners. Keep an inline summary only when errors are actionable in place.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`
- 🟡 **Inline styles, hardcoded color fallbacks, and glyph-as-icon bypass the token/icon system** _(M)_
  - Styling and iconography bypass the token and icon conventions. ra-intents uses literal color fallbacks var(--chs-color-success, green)/(..., red) (hardcoded non-WCAG colors) plus inline layout styles. ra-sod's render is full of inline style objects for layout/spacing/typography despite the file's own stated G6 'no new inline styles' invariant (they use tokens but remain inline). ra-role-editor uses a literal '▾' character as an expand/collapse icon (and lacks aria-expanded) instead of a Lucide currentColor <KitIcon>. ra-sod's severity color classes (chs-sodsev/chs-conflict) are custom, not the audited StatusChip, so AA contrast in both themes is unverified.
  - → Drop literal color fallbacks (rely on defined --chs-color-* tokens), move recurring inline layout/spacing/typography into the chs-* class layer or kit primitives per the G6 invariant, replace glyph-as-icon ('▾') with <KitIcon> (rotated, with aria-expanded), and verify or replace custom severity color classes with audited StatusChip/severity tokens meeting AA in light and dark.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`
- ⚪ **Russian microcopy and pluralization errors leak technical register** _(S)_
  - User-facing copy is grammatically wrong or mixes technical English into Russian admin text. ra-criticality hand-rolls Russian pluralization with a `<5` ternary that mishandles teens/21/hundreds ('11 запроса', '21 запроса'). ra-intents exposes internal vocabulary in outcome lines («статус ${state}», «(Tier-${tier})», «выдан TTL-грант», bare 'HTTP 500'). ra-sod's conflict-check copy is terse and doesn't distinguish 'unknown employee' from 'employee with no roles'.
  - → Use a proper plural helper (Intl.PluralRules('ru') one/few/many) for count labels, and map server enums/codes to plain-Russian phrases in one place — reserving technical terms (TTL, Tier) for tooltips/secondary text. Distinguish empty-result cases in copy.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-intents.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-sod.jsx`
- ⚪ **Code hygiene: index keys, unused imports, missing button type** _(S)_
  - Minor correctness/hygiene issues. ra-role-editor keys add/remove/reorderable grant and proposal lists by array index, which causes React to mismatch DOM/state on deletion — shifting open/focus state to the wrong row (a real glitch given rows contain expandable ScopePickers and focused inputs). ra-grant-trail imports SectionHead but never uses it. Several files have buttons without an explicit type (mostly false positives where the kit <Button> sets its own type, but the hand-rolled <button>s in ra-criticality and screen-rights should be defensively typed to avoid accidental form submission).
  - → Key grants/proposals by a stable id (uri or generated id) and track open index by that id; remove unused imports; and add type="button" to hand-rolled non-submit <button>s (the kit <Button> already manages its own type).
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/ra-role-editor.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-grant-trail.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/ra-criticality.jsx`, `src/screens/rights/screen-rights.jsx`
- ⚪ **Deep-link / selection fallback substitutes silently** _(S)_
  - In screen-rights, if an initialRole/sel value points at a role not present in the loaded set, the screen silently falls back to roles[0] with no indication — a deep-link to a removed or renamed role lands the user on an unrelated role without explanation.
  - → When a requested initialRole/sel cannot be resolved after load, surface a brief toast or inline note («Роль не найдена, показана первая») rather than silently substituting roles[0], so a stale deep-link doesn't mislead about which role is shown.
  - файлы: `src/screens/rights/screen-rights.jsx`

### shell-and-design-system (12 тем)

- 🔴 **Brandbook reference page crashes at runtime (missing ReactDOM import)** _(S)_
  - The design-system showcase — the canonical brandbook that every other screen is supposed to visually inherit from — imports only React but calls ReactDOM.createRoot, so it throws 'ReactDOM is not defined' on load and renders nothing. The single consistency reference for the whole app is non-functional.
  - → Add `import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client';` at the top of showcase.jsx and verify the page actually mounts in the dev build so it can serve as the consistency reference it claims to be.
  - файлы: `src/design/showcase.jsx`
- 🟠 **Custom interactive widgets are not keyboard-operable (listbox/clickable-div without roving focus or key handlers)** _(L)_
  - Multiple bespoke interactive surfaces advertise ARIA roles they don't deliver, locking out keyboard and screen-reader users. The command palette declares role=listbox with role=option children but has no arrow-key handling, no aria-activedescendant, and hardcodes aria-selected=false. RoleAssignment becomes clickable via onClick on a bare <div> with no tab order, no Enter/Space activation, no role and no focus ring (flagged critical by check_accessibility, WCAG 2.1.1). The showcase Popover menu items have no role=menu/menuitem or arrow-key navigation. All violate the contract's affordance/reachability rule.
  - → Implement real keyboard interaction for each surface: for the palette, track an active index, handle ArrowUp/Down/Enter on the input, set aria-activedescendant and aria-selected, and scroll the active option into view (or downgrade to a plain filtered link list). For RoleAssignment, render a real <button type=button> (or add role=button, tabIndex=0, aria-label and an Enter/Space onKeyDown) only when onOpen is set. For the showcase menu, add role=menu/menuitem with roving-tabindex arrow navigation to model the correct pattern.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/shell.jsx`, `src/components/components.jsx`, `src/design/showcase.jsx`
- 🟠 **Native alert() used instead of the toast/state convention for action feedback** _(M)_
  - downloadAuditLog surfaces all three failure paths (401, non-ok HTTP, network catch) via native window.alert(), and a successful export shows no feedback at all, so the user cannot distinguish success from silent failure. alert() is blocking, unstyled, untranslatable in tone and breaks consistent focus handling — explicitly flagged by the contract, which provides Toast/ToastViewport/useToasts for exactly this.
  - → Replace all alert() calls with kit toasts: emit a success toast on completed export and an assertive (role=alert) error toast on each failure branch. Mount one ToastViewport at the AppShell tree and pass the emitter down (or via context) so this and future actions share it.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/shell.jsx`
- 🟡 **Explicit-state primitives bypassed: bespoke loading/empty/error markup instead of LoadingState/EmptyState/ErrorState** _(M)_
  - The contract requires every loading/empty/error surface to use kit state primitives with the right ARIA live semantics, but the shell hand-rolls them. The first-paint auth-bootstrap gate renders a bare <div><p>Загрузка…</p> with no role=status/aria-busy (silent to screen readers). The palette no-results case is a plain <li> with no role=status, so the empty transition isn't announced. Auth bootstrap failures (tenant resolve, crumb-app fetch) are swallowed — org name silently shows «—» with no ErrorState/retry, conflating failure with a genuinely empty value. The export action also has no in-flight loading/busy state, inviting double-submits.
  - → Replace bespoke loading markup with kit <LoadingState>/<Spinner> (role=status aria-busy), render the palette empty result via <EmptyState> (or add role=status aria-live=polite), surface auth/tenant failures through an ErrorState-style alert with a «Повторить» affordance (distinguishing 'failed' from 'empty'), and add a busy/disabled state to the audit-export button via the kit <Button loading> prop with a finally block.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/shell.jsx`
- 🟡 **App-content controls hand-rolled as raw <button>/<input> instead of kit primitives** _(M)_
  - Several app-content interactive controls bypass the kit, losing its focus-ring, aria-disabled/aria-busy handling and visual-variant consistency — beyond the contract's narrow nav-only exception (ThemeToggle/NavItem). The shell logout «Выйти» is a bespoke <button> (and is mildly destructive, ending the session, with no confirmation); the command-palette filter is a raw <input> relying only on aria-label+placeholder instead of <Field> (placeholder-as-label disappears on input); the showcase Popover menu items are raw <button className=chs-menuitem> instead of the kit Button.
  - → Render app-content actions with the kit: logout as <Button variant=ghost/secondary> (optionally behind <ConfirmDialog> since it ends the session); wrap the palette filter in <Field> with a visually-hidden label; build menu items from the kit Button/menu-item primitive. Reserve raw <button> only for the sanctioned nav exception.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/shell.jsx`, `src/design/showcase.jsx`
- 🟡 **Disabled controls hide their reason in a tooltip keyboard/touch users cannot trigger** _(S)_
  - The honest-disabled topbar buttons («Исполнитель» / «Экспорт прав») carry their explanation («доступно владельцу» / «функция в разработке») only inside a wrapping Tooltip. A native disabled <button> is out of the tab order and receives no pointer events, so the tooltip never fires on keyboard focus and is unreliable on touch — the very reason for the disabled state is invisible to a large class of users. The intent (honest affordance) is good but the explanation is undiscoverable.
  - → Use aria-disabled=true (keeping the button focusable but inert via a handler guard) so the tooltip can fire on focus, or move the reason into always-visible helper text / a «скоро» inline badge next to the button. Wire the explanation via aria-describedby either way.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/shell.jsx`
- 🟡 **Overlay primitives lack focus management, robust dismissal and accessible naming** _(M)_
  - Kit overlays have accessibility and dismissal gaps. Modal/Drawer close on mousedown (target===currentTarget) rather than click, so a text-selection drag that releases on the backdrop dismisses the dialog — risking silent loss of unsaved input. Popover declares role=dialog but never moves focus into the panel, doesn't trap/restore focus, has no aria-label/labelledby and no aria-expanded/aria-controls on the trigger, so it's announced ambiguously. Tooltip wraps a generic focusable <span> (redundant tab stop), shows the bubble via CSS hover/focus only with no Esc-to-dismiss and uncertain persistence, risking WCAG 1.4.13.
  - → Switch backdrop dismissal to onClick (and gate dirty-form Modals behind a dirty-state check / ConfirmDialog). Bring Popover to dialog parity (focus first focusable on open, restore on close, accept a labelId/aria-label, add aria-expanded/aria-controls) — or downgrade it to a lightweight non-dialog menu. For Tooltip, put aria-describedby on the already-interactive child instead of a separate span, ensure the bubble stays hoverable and add Esc-to-hide.
  - файлы: `src/components/components.jsx`
- 🟡 **Decorative-glyph and missing-fallback gaps in the icon layer** _(M)_
  - Icon usage deviates from the Lucide/currentColor contract in two ways. The showcase Popover trigger uses the Unicode caret «▾» as a dropdown affordance — a glyph character (same class as banned ✓/✗) that won't inherit icon sizing/stroke/color tokens and is read aloud as 'black down-pointing small triangle'. Separately, the Icon factory is a flat chain of `name === ... &&` branches with no default, so an unknown/typo'd name renders a silent blank 16x16 box with no dev warning and no visible placeholder — a missing glyph is indistinguishable from a layout gap and easy to ship.
  - → Replace «▾» with <KitIcon name=chevron-down aria-hidden>. In icon.jsx add a default branch that renders a visible fallback glyph for unknown names plus a dev-only console.warn, and consider a data-driven Set/map lookup instead of the 20-deep boolean chain.
  - файлы: `src/design/showcase.jsx`, `src/app-shell/icon.jsx`
- 🟡 **No global Toast provider; auto-dismiss timing harms error recoverability** _(M)_
  - The toast system lacks a provider/context (the contract itself lists this as a gap), so every React tree must independently call useToasts() and mount a ToastViewport, and deep components must prop-drill the push fn. This directly causes the inconsistent action-feedback seen elsewhere (some actions toast, some silently succeed, the shell falls back to alert()). Additionally all toasts — including tone=error (role=alert) — auto-dismiss after the same 4000ms with no pause-on-hover/focus, so a failed save/delete error can vanish before it's read, and the per-toast timer isn't cleared on manual dismiss.
  - → Add a ToastProvider + useToastContext (or a singleton toast store) with one documented app-root ToastViewport so any component can push without local wiring. Default error/warning toasts to no auto-dismiss (duration 0) or pause the timer on pointerenter/focus, and clear the per-toast timeout in dismiss().
  - файлы: `src/components/components.jsx`
- ⚪ **Hardcoded sizes/styles bypass design tokens** _(S)_
  - Multiple files bake in raw pixel/size values instead of consuming --chs-* tokens, ironically including the file whose job is to model disciplined token usage. The Icon factory hardcodes width=16/height=16 and strokeWidth=1.6 with no token-driven sizing hook, forcing callers to fight explicit attributes for multi-size use. BudgetMeter sets a static font-size/color label via an inline style object rather than a class (unlike ReservationMeter's chs-resv__label). The showcase uses raw px in inline styles (marginBottom:6, width:60, height:48, gap:8, minWidth:160), including on the very tiles that showcase token-based shadows.
  - → Drop fixed width/height on Icon (default to 1em / size prop, keep viewBox) so size flows from font-size; move the BudgetMeter label to a chs-budget__label class on tokens; and replace raw px in showcase inline styles with var(--chs-space-*)/sizing tokens, keeping only genuinely dynamic width%/left% inline.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/icon.jsx`, `src/components/components.jsx`, `src/design/showcase.jsx`
- ⚪ **Bespoke buttons omit explicit type="button" (latent submit footgun)** _(S)_
  - review_code flagged numerous hand-rolled <button> elements across the shell (ThemeToggle x2, NavItem, nav search, logout) and the showcase (theme-toggle x2, Popover menu items) that omit an explicit type, unlike the kit <Button> which always sets it. Harmless in the current non-form context, but any such button moved inside a <form> defaults to type=submit and triggers unintended submission — and it's an inconsistency with the kit contract.
  - → Add type="button" to every bespoke <button> in the shell and showcase. Low effort, clears the warning class and removes the latent submit bug.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/shell.jsx`, `src/design/showcase.jsx`
- ⚪ **Icon-only controls and toggle state risk color-only / unlabeled signaling** _(S)_
  - Two minor non-text-content risks. The Icon component is hardcoded aria-hidden=true with no path to expose an accessible name when used as the sole content of an interactive control (icon-only menu/search button), so callers must remember to label the wrapping button or a screen reader announces an unlabeled button (WCAG 1.1.1). ThemeToggle correctly uses aria-pressed, but its selected/unselected distinction is delivered via CSS not visible in-file; if conveyed by color/background alone it risks WCAG 1.4.1 (Use of Color).
  - → Give Icon an optional title/label prop that, when set, drops aria-hidden, adds role=img and renders a <title> (default stays decorative) — or loudly document that icon-only buttons must supply their own aria-label. Verify the ThemeToggle pressed segment has a non-color affordance (filled background + border or check) with sufficient contrast in both themes.
  - файлы: `src/app-shell/icon.jsx`, `src/app-shell/shell.jsx`

---

## Подробно по файлам (48)

### `src/screens/screen-agents.jsx` — 🟠2 🟡1 ⚪3
_Lists a tenant's AI agents and lets the user connect a new agent, bind an LLM via a secret-handle, pick a named LLM connection per agent, and inspect each agent's activity log._

- 🟠 **Auto-save LLM-connection select gives no live-announced feedback on success or failure** (feedback-state · _WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA)_ · `AgentRow saveConnection + <Select> hint, screen-agents.jsx:210-302`)
  - Changing the LLM-connection select fires an immediate PUT with no explicit save action. The only feedback is the select's `hint` text cycling through 'Сохраняю…' / 'Сохранено.' / error. That hint is wired via aria-describedby, but a describedby string that changes is not reliably re-announced by screen readers, and there is no role=status/alert live region. A keyboard or SR user who silently mutates an agent's model binding gets no confirmation, and a failed save (which silently reverts connId) is also unannounced — the value snaps back with no spoken reason. The design-system contract mandates explicit feedback for every action (Toast for transient outcome).
  - → On success emit a transient kit <Toast> via useToasts() (status/polite) e.g. 'LLM-подключение сохранено', and on failure a Toast (alert/assertive). At minimum wrap the saving/saved/error hint in an aria-live region (role=status for progress/success, role=alert for error) so the outcome is announced. Since the failure silently reverts the selection, the error announcement is essential.
- 🟠 **Form submit-error banners are not announced (no role=alert)** (accessibility · _WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA)_ · `HireModal submitErr div:421; BindModal submitErr div:511; ActivityPanel inline error div:170; bannerErrStyle:48`)
  - Server/network submit errors render in a plain styled <div> with no role=alert and no aria-live. After a failed 'Подключить'/'Привязать' submit, focus stays on the submit button and the error appears silently below — a screen-reader user gets no notification that the action failed or why. Same for the ActivityPanel 'load more' inline error. The contract's state conventions require Error=role=alert.
  - → Give the submit-error banner role="alert" (assertive) so it is announced on appearance. Reuse the kit <ErrorState> styling or an <Toast> (error=alert) instead of a bespoke bannerErrStyle div for consistency with the rest of the kit.
- 🟡 **Raw agent slug surfaced as a primary identifier in each row** (content-clarity · _no dev-jargon in UI_ · `AgentRow:253 <MonoId>{agent.slug}</MonoId>`)
  - Each agent row shows agent.slug (e.g. 'recon-bot') in mono as a secondary identifier line. While a slug is more human than a UUID, the contract calls out 'no dev-jargon / code-names' in the UI, and the hire form explicitly teaches the slug as 'строчные латинские, цифры, дефис' — i.e. a technical handle. Leading the secondary line with this machine handle (rather than the org-place / role) lowers scannability for non-technical B2B operators, who think in terms of position/department, not slugs.
  - → Lead the secondary line with the human org-place (position · department) and demote the slug to a muted suffix or a tooltip/Tooltip on the name, or drop it from the list entirely and show it only in a detail view. If kept, keep it clearly subordinate to the role text.
- ⚪ **Activity disclosure button missing aria-controls linking it to the panel** (accessibility · _WCAG 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value (A) — disclosure pattern_ · `AgentRow activity toggle Button:269-276 and panel div:324`)
  - The 'Активность' toggle correctly sets aria-expanded, but the expanded region (the ActivityPanel container at line 324) has no id and the button has no aria-controls, so assistive tech cannot associate the trigger with the region it controls. The expanded panel is also not focus-managed.
  - → Add a stable id to the activity panel container and aria-controls={panelId} on the toggle Button. Optionally give the panel region role="region" with an aria-label so it is reachable/identifiable when expanded.
- ⚪ **Destructive/irreversible-ish nature of overwriting an agent's LLM binding has no confirmation** (ux-pattern · _destructive-action confirmation_ · `AgentRow LLM-connection <Select> onChange=saveConnection:210-302`)
  - Selecting a different LLM connection (or '— Не задано —') immediately re-binds the agent's brain with a single, undoable-only-by-reselect interaction and no confirmation. For a running agent this silently changes which model/credentials it executes on. It is not strictly destructive, but it is a consequential change applied on a stray change event with no commit step and (per finding 1) no clear confirmation.
  - → This is acceptable as inline-save IF paired with a clear confirmation toast (finding 1). If switching the binding can disrupt in-flight work, consider a lightweight ConfirmDialog when moving an already-bound agent to a different/empty connection, or an explicit 'Сохранить' affordance instead of change-to-commit.
- ⚪ **First-page activity-load error suppresses the partial-failure path; combined null/empty/loading states are subtly racy** (feedback-state · _loading/empty/error state machine_ · `ActivityPanel render guards:125-144`)
  - The state guards are mostly honest, but the order/conditions leave a thin gap: if a refetch is triggered while items is non-null and fails, the inline error (line 169) only renders when items.length>0; a successful-then-emptied list that later errors could show the EmptyState without the error context. Minor, but the 'first page failed' path sets items=[] AND error, then relies on the error-branch ordering to win — readable but fragile if the guards are reordered.
  - → Keep as-is functionally, but consider a single derived status (loading|error|empty|ready) to make the precedence explicit and prevent future regressions where empty masks an error.

### `src/screens/screen-assistant-prompt.jsx` — 🟠2 🟡5 ⚪2
_Editor for tenant-level system prompts of the analyst and configurator assistant roles, saved as drafts that take effect only after a separate promote step._

- 🟠 **Bespoke <textarea> instead of design-system field wrapper; success/error banners hand-rolled instead of Toast** (consistency · _design-system primitive consumption_ · `screen-assistant-prompt.jsx:234-241 (textarea), :247-248 (banners)`)
  - The contract names a textarea/Field gap and says feedback after an action should use the Toast convention (<Toast> + useToasts + role/aria-live). Here the main editing control is a raw <textarea> with a hand-styled `textareaStyle` object, and save success/failure are rendered as bespoke <div> banners (bannerOkStyle/bannerErrStyle) with no aria-live region. This is exactly the 'rolled-its-own input / bare banner instead of state primitive' flag, and it means save outcomes are not announced to screen readers and look different from feedback elsewhere in the app.
  - → Wrap the textarea in the Field pattern (label↔control binding, aria-describedby for the hint) or extend the kit with a textarea wrapper; route save success/failure through useToasts()+<ToastViewport> (error=alert/assertive, success=status/polite) instead of static <div> banners, or at minimum give the banners role=alert (error) / role=status (success) so outcomes are announced.
- 🟠 **Promote step is described but has no in-product affordance — workflow dead-ends** (flow · _task completion / affordance rule_ · `screen-assistant-prompt.jsx:243-248, :326-348`)
  - The screen lets the user save a draft and then repeatedly tells them the prompt only takes effect after 'promote', but there is no button or link to actually promote — the user is instructed to call POST /api/artifacts/promote themselves. The core job (make a new prompt live) cannot be completed on this screen, so every successful save ends in a dead-end. This violates the affordance principle (an enabled flow must lead somewhere) and the success toast points at an API the user cannot invoke from the UI.
